Online booking confirmations are now always emailed, whatever the patient's email choice, and say "If you did not book it, please contact the clinic"; `notifyPatient` gains `alwaysEmail` and logs a Resend refusal with `console.error`; `lib/booking/follow-up.ts` gains `patientWasTold` and `tellTeamPatientNotTold`, which `bookOnline` calls so admins and PAs get a second notice when the confirmation reached the patient by neither email nor WhatsApp. This follows the clinic's decision of 12 November 2026 to keep online bookings without a confirm link, which the caller reports is recorded on branch `docs/diary-decisions`, not yet merged (Finding 205). ## 1. Product summary

Clinic Hub is the patient and clinic portal pair for The Schoeman Clinic (hormone, longevity and functional medicine, London). The MVP is built around the bloods loop: order, Stripe payment and barcode, results by lab feed or PDF import with PA review, doctor review and release, then the patient sees the trend. GoHighLevel keeps messaging, reminders, tasks, calendar, invoices and marketing. Semble keeps consultation notes and prescribing for now. [2026-11] Since the clinic's decisions of 11 November, clinical notes are written in Clinic Hub too (drafts, signed and locked, corrected by a new version; no Semble sync), staff assign education courses to patients, and patients type in wearable readings (Finding 74 on the Semble wording). Prescribing goes through SignatureRx per `CLAUDE.md` and is not built. Each staff member can connect their own Google Calendar and see and edit it at `/clinic/calendar`. [2026-11, `feat/clinic-build-2`] Since the clinic's decisions of 11 and 12 November: **appointments are booked in Clinic Hub** (patients with their care team's clinicians, staff for any patient they can open, inside each clinician's weekly hours; the booking goes into the clinician's Google Calendar if connected; patients cannot cancel, the team does); a **signed note is final** (no corrections); a doctor, nurse or health coach **records a call** from the patient's record and the AssemblyAI transcript arrives as a draft note (replacing Fathom); and patient messages (result ready, appointment booked or cancelled) go **by email and WhatsApp through GoHighLevel** when it is connected, otherwise by email through Resend. Bookings are not synced to or from GoHighLevel or Semble (Finding 89). [2026-11, `feat/finalise-points`] Patients (and guardians who can act) **ask the clinic** questions in the portal and the conversation continues in replies on both sides; the team **upload and share letters, referrals and other documents**, which patients open in the portal; patients record **consent choices** (care, partner laboratories, research); and the clinic's patient record shows allergies, diagnoses, medicines (read only), documents, open questions and, for doctors, safeguarding alerts, with a doctors-only safeguarding list. Questions replace the draft's plan of replying in GoHighLevel (Finding 112). [2026-11, `feat/clinic-diary`, `4f3c29c`] Staff have a **clinic diary** of every clinician's bookings by day, week or month, with patient names shown to the whole team (opening a record still follows the care team), and can move a Clinic Hub booking to another time or clinician. **New patients book online** at the public page `/book` instead of through GoHighLevel calendars: Clinic Hub creates their patient record (with no portal account) and the booking together, emails a confirmation and tells admins and PAs, who check the details and invite them.

Postgres 17 (`major_version = 17`). 34 tables in `public` (33 before `google_calendar_connections` [2026-11]), plus `private.auth_throttle` (see 4.15); `auth.users` is managed by Supabase Auth and only referenced. Every table has `created_at` [2026-11], except `google_calendar_connections`, which has `connected_at` and `updated_at` instead (Finding 63). Types are generated into `lib/database.types.ts` with `pnpm db:types`.

### 4.1 Principles

- Row-level security is **enabled and forced** on every table in `public` (loop at the end of the integrations migration). No `FOR ALL` policies. Each table has **one permissive policy per command**, combining roles with OR, so the `multiple_permissive_policies` advisor stays quiet [2026-11].
- All new objects start closed: default privileges revoke everything from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated`. The grants migration then revokes and re-grants explicitly. `anon` holds nothing (there are no public pages). Column-level grants are used for insert and update.
- No `ON DELETE CASCADE` or `SET NULL` anywhere (every foreign key is `on delete restrict`, checked by a test). `authenticated` can delete only from `staff_locations` and `lab_panel_markers`. The service role has **no delete or truncate** on any clinical table, including `profiles`, `user_roles`, `webhook_events` and `integration_sync_log` [2026-11]. Clinical records are archived with `archived_at`; accounts are suspended through `profiles.status`.
- `webhook_events`, `integration_sync_log` and `audit_log` have no policies and no grants to `authenticated`: no table access. Since `20261110120100` [2026-11] two functions reach `audit_log` for signed-in people: `log_record_view` writes a `viewed` row (anyone with the patient in scope since `20261112120100` [2026-11]) and `activity_log` lets an admin read it (4.7c).
- Policies call helpers as `(select private.has_role('x'))` and `(select auth.uid())` so Postgres evaluates them once per statement. Policies that take a row value (`private.in_scope(patient_id)`, `can_act_for`, `document_gate_open`) are called directly.
- Every function sets `search_path`. No `SECURITY DEFINER` function exists in `public`; a test enforces it.
- Writes that must not come from a browser are made by server code with the service role (`lib/supabase/admin.ts`), after the server has checked who is asking. Several tables therefore have a select policy only.

### 4.2 Roles and access levels

`app_role` is `patient, doctor, pa, health_coach, nurse, admin` (`nurse` added in `20261110120000` [2026-11]). There is **no guardian role** [2026-11]: a guardian holds no role and is defined by a verified `patient_guardians` row (`verified_by` not null) with `can_view` (and `can_act` to pay, request or ask). A person can hold more than one role (the seed has a doctor who is also a patient).

| Group | Roles | Helper |
|---|---|---|
| Clinical staff | doctor, **nurse** (since `20261112120100` [2026-11]), PA, health coach | `private.is_clinical_staff()` |
| Team | doctor, PA, admin | `private.is_team()` |
| Admin | admin | `private.has_role('admin')` |
| Who may open a patient's record | PAs and admins: every patient. Doctors, nurses and health coaches: patients whose care team they are currently on | `private.in_scope(patient_id)` |

**Access by care team** [2026-11, `20261112120100_access_by_care_team.sql`, the clinic's answers to knowledge base questions 1 to 3 and 7]. `private.in_scope()` is no longer "any clinical staff": it is true for an admin or a PA for every patient, and for a doctor, nurse or health coach only while a `care_team` row links their staff profile to the patient with `since <= current_date` and `until` empty or after today (so setting `until` to today removes access at once). Because every patient-record policy already called `in_scope`, this one change narrows them all. Inside their scope, staff read the **whole record**, including unreleased reports, results and documents (the release gate now admits anyone in scope, 4.5). Still limited by role on top: safeguarding alerts (doctors in scope), diagnoses and medications (written by doctors), release of results (doctors), care-team edits (PA, admin, doctor), clinical notes (written by doctors, nurses and health coaches).

**Nurse** [2026-11]: since `20261112120100` the nurse is clinical staff and works like a doctor on the patients of their care team, except safeguarding, diagnoses, prescribing and release: the nurse reads the record, enters results and sends files for entry (`enter_report`, `register_import`), works the import queue, adds and changes allergies, adds and changes `documents` rows, and writes clinical notes. A nurse can be put on a care team (`care_relationship` value `nurse`, `20261112120000`). The `documents` **storage** insert policy was not widened, so a nurse cannot upload the file itself (Finding 71).

**Admin** [2026-11]: **an admin now reads every patient's clinical record** (`in_scope` is true for admins), including unreleased results, documents and signed notes, as the clinic decided (knowledge base questions 2 and 27). An admin is still not clinical staff: no role-specific write (diagnoses, medications, release, notes) opens to them, but they edit care teams, assign education courses and change question status (`is_team()` and `in_scope`). The earlier rule "admin reads no patient record" no longer holds; neither does "the health coach reads released results and documents only": a health coach on a patient's care team reads everything an in-scope reader reads except safeguarding alerts, and every care-team member reads that patient's care team (`is_team() or in_scope`). The health coach still reads no staff profiles, import queue, invitations or staff locations.

**Own record [2026-11, `a55db7b`, edit to `20261112120100`]:** `in_scope` is false for the caller's own patient record and for any patient in their `my_patient_ids()` (people they look after), so a team member who is also a patient sees their own record only as a patient does (S-112). A trigger, `care_team_reject_self`, refuses putting a staff member on their own care team. The care-team read policy is now `in_scope(patient_id)` alone (the `is_team()` half was dropped).

A staff role counts only while the person has an active, unarchived `staff_profiles` row and the account is not suspended, so offboarding takes effect immediately without editing roles. A patient role also needs a non-suspended account.

Access levels by table: own account (profiles, notification tables), patient record (clinical staff in scope plus the patient and verified guardians), team only, doctors only (safeguarding alerts; diagnoses and medications are doctor-written), reference (readable by anyone signed in, admin-written), server only.

### 4.3 Enums (`20261102111631_enums.sql`)

| Enum | Values |
|---|---|
| `app_role` | patient, doctor, pa, health_coach, nurse, admin (`nurse` added in `20261110120000` [2026-11]) |
| `account_status` | invited, active, suspended |
| `staff_role` | doctor, pa, health_coach, nurse, admin (`nurse` added in `20261110120000` [2026-11]) |
| `diagnosis_status` | active, monitoring, treating, under_investigation, resolved |
| `med_kind` | prescription, supplement |
| `med_status` | active, paused, stopped |
| `request_status` | requested, approved, sent_to_pharmacy, declined |
| `appt_status` | booked, confirmed, cancelled, completed, no_show |
| `doc_kind` | letter, lab_report, patient_upload, referral, other |
| `question_status` | awaiting_reply, answered, closed |
| `order_status` | sent_to_patient, paid, sample_taken, results_in, reviewed, cancelled |
| `report_status` | awaiting_review, reviewed, released |
| `result_flag` | normal, borderline, low, high, critical |
| `import_status` | needs_match, matched, confirmed, rejected |
| `notification_topic` | results, payments, appointments, messages |
| `notification_channel` | email, whatsapp, sms, portal |
| `location_kind` | clinic, video |
| `care_relationship` | primary_doctor, doctor, pa, health_coach, nurse (`nurse` added in `20261112120000` [2026-11]) |
| `guardian_relationship` | parent, carer, power_of_attorney |
| `consent_type` | care, partner_labs, research |
| `safeguarding_severity` | low, moderate, high |
| `appt_source` | ghl, semble, clinic_hub (`clinic_hub` added alone in `20261113120400` [2026-11]: a booking made in Clinic Hub) |
| `document_source` | patient_upload, email, tdl, semble, staff_upload (added in `20261109120000` [2026-11]), lab_feed (added in `20261122120000` [2026-11, `feat/lab-webhook`]) |
| `question_urgency` | routine, important |
| `results_channel` | api, email, upload |
| `import_confidence` | high, medium, low |
| `import_decision` | pending, confirmed, rejected |
| `webhook_source` | stripe, tdl, ghl, semble, fathom, lab_feed (added in `20261122120000` [2026-11, `feat/lab-webhook`]) |
| `webhook_status` | received, processed, failed, ignored |
| `sync_system` | ghl, semble, stripe, tdl, fathom |
| `sync_direction` | in, out |
| `sync_status` | ok, failed, retrying |
| `invitation_kind` | patient, guardian, team (made in `20261105120000_invitations.sql`, so 33 enums in all) [2026-11] |
| `note_status` | draft, signed (`20261112120300` [2026-11]) |
| `note_source` | typed, recording (`20261112120300` [2026-11]; `recording` is a call transcript checked before signing, written by `recording_transcribed` since `20261113120600` [2026-11]) |
| `wearable_metric` | sleep_minutes, sleep_score, readiness_score, resting_heart_rate, hrv_ms, steps, active_minutes, weight_kg, body_fat_percent, vo2_max, spo2_percent, temperature_deviation_c (`20261112120400` [2026-11]; 36 enums in all) |
| `recording_status` | uploading, transcribing, transcribed, failed (`20261113120600` [2026-11]; 37 enums in all) |
| `form_kind` | feedback, repeat_request, question (`20261117120000` [2026-11]; 38 enums in all). Which form a `form_submissions` row copies |

[2026-11] The `fathom` values of `webhook_source` and `sync_system` are kept but unused: Fathom is replaced by AssemblyAI, which has no enum value and writes nothing to `webhook_events` or `integration_sync_log` (Finding 101).

The 17 from `notification_topic` to `sync_status` were the first extra enums for fixed lists that the draft left as text; every enum added since (from `invitation_kind` to `form_kind`) is also outside the draft [2026-11]. Original wording: the last 17 are extra enums for fixed lists that the draft left as text [2026-11].

### 4.4 Tables

`id` is `uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid()` unless stated. `created_at timestamptz not null default now()` exists on every table and is not repeated below. "Server" means written only by server code with the service role (no write policy for `authenticated`).

**Identity and accounts** (`20261102111657_identity_and_accounts.sql`)

- `profiles`: `id` (pk, fk `auth.users`), `full_name` (default ''), `email`, `phone`, `avatar_path`, `locale` ('en-GB'), `status account_status` ('invited'), `last_seen_at`, `updated_at`. Select: own or team. Update: own or admin, via trigger rules below. Created by the `on_auth_user_created` trigger.
- `user_roles`: pk `(user_id, role)`; `user_id` fk profiles; `role app_role`; `granted_by` fk profiles (check: not the person themselves); `granted_at`. Select: own or admin. Write: server.
- `invitations`: `email`, `kind invitation_kind` (patient, guardian, team), `role` (nullable: a guardian has none), `patient_id` fk patient_profiles, `relationship guardian_relationship`, `can_act`, `job_title`, `full_name` (check: 120 characters), `invited_by` fk staff_profiles, `token_hash` (check length >= 32; unique index; only a SHA-256 of the 43-character link token), `sent_at`, `expires_at` (check: within 14 days of creation; the functions set 3 days), `accepted_at`, `failed_attempts` (smallint, default 0: wrong dates of birth for this link; the fifth cancels it, `20261105130000` [2026-11]), `revoked_at` (a cancelled invitation, or one replaced by a resend; check: not both accepted and revoked). `invitations_kind_fields` says what each kind carries: patient = role `patient` + patient; guardian = no role + patient + relationship + `can_act`; team = a team role (doctor, pa, health_coach, admin) and no patient. One pending invitation (not accepted, not cancelled) per `lower(email)`; an expired one is cancelled by the next invitation to that address. Select: admins read all, PAs read patient and guardian invitations for patients in `in_scope`, and since `20261112120200` [2026-11] a doctor reads the patient invitations whose `invited_by` is their own staff profile; column grants exclude `token_hash`. **Created, resent, cancelled and accepted only through the functions in 4.7a** [2026-11].
- `notification_preferences`: pk `(user_id, topic, channel)`; `enabled`; `updated_at`. Own select, insert, update (`enabled` only).
- `notifications`: `user_id`, `topic`, `title`, `body`, `link`, `read_at`. Own select; update `read_at` only. Created by the server.

**Team and sites** (`20261102111701_team_and_sites.sql`)

- `locations`: `name`, `kind location_kind`, `address`, `ghl_calendar_id`. Anyone signed in reads; admin writes.
- `staff_profiles`: `user_id` (unique, fk profiles), `job_title`, `clinical_role staff_role`, `gmc_number` (doctors only, check), `signature_path` (must start with own user id, check), `ghl_user_id`, `semble_user_id`, `active` (true), `archived_at`. Select: own or team. Insert and most updates: admin; a staff member may change only their own `signature_path`. Triggers lock `id`, `user_id`, `ghl_user_id`, `semble_user_id` from clients. [2026-11, `20261114120000`] Trigger `staff_profiles_drop_leaver_connections` (after update of `active` or `archived_at`): when an active, unarchived profile becomes inactive or archived, the person's `google_calendar_connections` row is deleted, logged as system job `offboarding` (Finding 64).
- `staff_locations`: pk `(staff_id, location_id)`; `is_primary`. Team reads; admin writes (including delete).
- `care_team`: `patient_id` fk, `staff_id` fk, `relationship care_relationship`, `since` (today), `until` (check >= since). Unique current primary doctor per patient. **Since `20261112120100` [2026-11] it decides access** for doctors, nurses and health coaches (`in_scope`, 4.2). Select: the team (`is_team()`) or anyone with the patient in scope. Insert and update: PA, admin or doctor, with the patient in scope (so a doctor edits only the care teams they are on). No delete grant: leaving is `until` set to today (`removeFromCareTeam`). [2026-11, `8399375`] `since` defaults to London-time today, and `in_scope` compares `since` and `until` with `private.london_today()`. [2026-11, `20261114120000`] Trigger `care_team_role_matches` (before insert, or update of `staff_id` or `relationship`): the relationship must match the person's `clinical_role` (`primary_doctor` and `doctor` need a doctor, `nurse` a nurse, `pa` a PA, `health_coach` a health coach), else 22023 "That role on the care team does not match the person's own role."

**Patient record** (`20261102111704_patient_record.sql`)

- `patient_profiles`: `user_id` (unique, nullable until the invitation is accepted), `clinic_ref` (unique), `first_name`, `last_name`, `date_of_birth`, `sex` (**sex at birth** [2026-11]: decides blood panels and cycle guidance; a missing value must stop those steps, never be guessed), `gender_identity` (text, nullable, at most 60 characters, check `patient_profiles_gender_identity_length`; the patient's own words, held beside sex and never instead of it; insert and update granted to `authenticated` by column, `20261110120100`), `contact_email`, `contact_phone`, `country`, `primary_clinician_id` fk staff_profiles, `ghl_contact_id`, `semble_patient_id`, `stripe_customer_id`, `archived_at`, `updated_at`. Select: staff with the patient in scope (since `20261112120100` [2026-11]: PAs and admins all, doctors, nurses and coaches their care team's; before, any clinical staff and no admin). Patients and guardians read it through `get_my_patient_record()`, which omits external ids. Insert and update: PA. Trigger locks `id`, `user_id`, `clinic_ref` and the three external ids. [2026-11, `20261113120300`] `ghl_contact_id` is now written by the server through `set_patient_ghl_contact` (4.7h) the first time a patient is messaged through GoHighLevel. [2026-11, `29f602f`, edit to `20261113120300`] Unique partial index `patient_profiles_ghl_contact_unique` (where not null): two patients can never share a GoHighLevel contact; a clash makes `set_patient_ghl_contact` fail and `notifyPatient` falls back to Resend for that patient. [2026-11, `20261119120000`] New column `booked_online_at timestamptz` (nullable, no grant to `authenticated`): set by `book_online` when a new patient's record is made on the public booking page; the team checks the details and invites them. With `user_id` null it makes the diary's `new_patient` flag.
- `patient_guardians`: `patient_id`, `guardian_user_id` fk profiles, `relationship guardian_relationship`, `can_view` (true), `can_act` (false, check: act needs view), `verified_by` fk staff_profiles. Unique `(patient_id, guardian_user_id)`. Select: the guardian, the patient or staff in scope. Write: PA only; cannot link themselves; may verify only in their own name. [2026-11, `20261113120200`] Update: a **PA or an admin** with the patient in scope, never on a link to themselves; a link may be verified only in the writer's own name, but **any PA or admin may remove one** (set `can_view` and `can_act` both false) whoever verified it. Removal is the only way a guardian loses access; nothing is deleted, and a patient cannot remove a guardian. Insert is still server-only (`accept_invitation`). [2026-11, `20261114120000`] Trigger `patient_guardians_guard_verifier` (before update): for `authenticated`, `verified_by` may change only to the writer's own staff id, so removing a link cannot rename its verifier (S-125). A removed guardian can be invited again: `create_invitation` now refuses only when a link with `can_view` or `can_act` exists, and `accept_invitation` turns the old row back on (`on conflict … do update`: relationship, `can_view` true, `can_act` as invited, `verified_by` the inviter).
- `patient_allergies`: `patient_id`, `substance`, `reaction`, `severity` (text), `recorded_by` fk staff_profiles. Read: patient record level. Write: doctor and PA, `recorded_by` must be the writer. [2026-11] Since `20261112120100` the nurse may add and change allergies too, in scope (policy names still say "Doctors and PAs"). **[2026-11, `20261115120000`]** `archived_at` (timestamptz, null): set when an allergy was entered by mistake; `authenticated` gains `update (archived_at)`, under the existing update policy (doctor, nurse or PA, in scope). The select policy does not filter archived rows; screens do (`getClinicalSummary`). Audit trigger as before.
- `consents`: `patient_id`, `consent_type`, `version`, `granted`, `changed_at`. Insert only (a change is a new row), by anyone who can act for the patient.
- `diagnoses`: `patient_id`, `icd10_code`, `label`, `status diagnosis_status` ('active'), `diagnosed_on`, `recorded_by`, `notes`, `archived_at`. Patients read unarchived ones. Doctors write.
- `safeguarding_alerts`: `patient_id`, `alert_type`, `severity`, `note`, `added_by`, `review_due`, `status` (text, 'active'). Doctors only, for read and write.
- `medications`: `patient_id`, `name`, `dose`, `instructions`, `kind med_kind`, `prescriber_id`, `pharmacy`, `start_date`, `supply_days` (check > 0), `status med_status` ('active'), `semble_prescription_id`, `archived_at`. Unique `(id, patient_id)` to back the composite key. Patients read unarchived ones. Doctors write. Trigger locks `id`, `patient_id`, `semble_prescription_id`. [2026-11, `20261116120000`] `run_out_reminded_for date`: the run-out day the patient was last reminded about; written only by `mark_run_out_reminded` (not in the `authenticated` update grant).

**Care and requests** (`20261102111707_care_and_requests.sql`)

- `repeat_requests`: `medication_id`, `patient_id`, `requested_by` fk profiles, `note`, `fee_pence` (3000, check >= 0), `stripe_payment_intent_id`, `status request_status` ('requested'), `ghl_task_id`, `requested_at`, `resolved_at`. **Composite fk `(medication_id, patient_id)` to `medications (id, patient_id)`**, so the medication belongs to the same patient. Insert: someone who can act for the patient, or a PA; `requested_by` must be the caller. Update (`status`, `resolved_at`): PA and doctor. **[2026-11, `20261116120000`]** `medication_id` is now nullable (kept for requests made before; newer ones list their medicines in `repeat_request_items`). New columns: `has_future_appointment`, `wants_appointment`, `still_losing_weight`, `has_side_effects` (booleans, nullable), `weight_lost_last_month` (at most 200), `side_effects` (at most 2,000), `paid_at`, `stripe_checkout_session_id` (unique), `cancelled_at`, `decided_by` fk profiles (restrict), `decision_note` (at most 2,000); `note` now at most 2,000; unique `(id, patient_id)` to back the items' composite key; indexes on `decided_by` and on `paid_at desc` where paid. **The insert and update policies are dropped and the insert and update grants revoked**: `authenticated` has select only, and requests are made, withdrawn, decided and paid only through the functions in 4.7j. The read policy is unchanged.
- `prescribable_medicines` [2026-11, `20261116120000`]: `name` (1 to 120 after trimming; unique on `lower(btrim(name))` among unarchived rows), `weight_loss` (false), `sort_order` (0), `archived_at`, `created_at`; no `updated_at`. Seeded by the migration itself (not `seed.sql`) with the clinic Typeform's 24 medicines, four marked weight loss (Mounjaro, Ozempic, Semaglutide, Wegovy (semaglutide)). Read: anyone signed in sees unarchived rows; admins and doctors also archived ones. Insert (`name`, `weight_loss`, `sort_order`) and update (those and `archived_at`): admins and doctors. No delete. Audited.
- `repeat_request_items` [2026-11, `20261116120000`]: `request_id`, `patient_id` fk patient_profiles, `medication_id`, `medicine_id` fk prescribable_medicines, `other_name` (1 to 200 after trimming), `supply_months` (smallint, 1 or 2), `created_at`. Check: exactly one of `medication_id`, `medicine_id`, `other_name`. **Composite fks `(request_id, patient_id)` to `repeat_requests (id, patient_id)` and `(medication_id, patient_id)` to `medications (id, patient_id)`**, so every line belongs to the request's patient; all restrict. Read: the patient side (`my_patient_ids`) or staff in scope. No write grant or policy for `authenticated`: rows come only from `request_repeat`. Audited.
- `appointments`: `patient_id`, `clinician_id`, `location_id`, `starts_at`, `duration_min` (check > 0), `type` (text), `status appt_status` ('booked'), `source appt_source`, `external_id`, `synced_at`. Unique `(source, external_id)`. Read: patient record level. No write policy: the sync writes. **No notes column.** [2026-11, `20261113120500`] New columns `appointment_type_id` (fk `appointment_types`, nullable) and `booked_by` (fk profiles, nullable: who booked it in Clinic Hub; empty for copied bookings), both indexed. Still no insert or update grant for `authenticated`: Clinic Hub bookings are written by `book_appointment` and cancelled by `cancel_appointment` (4.7f), with `source = 'clinic_hub'`, `type` = the appointment type's name and `duration_min` from the type. For a `clinic_hub` row, `external_id` is the **Google Calendar event id** in the clinician's own calendar (set by `set_appointment_calendar_event`), not a GoHighLevel or Semble id. [2026-11, `20261119120000`] New column `booked_online boolean not null default false`, written only by `book_online` (no grant to `authenticated`). Moves go through `move_appointment` (4.7n), which changes `clinician_id`, `starts_at` and `location_id` (~~and sets `status` back to `booked`~~: since `49cb8b8` the status is kept).
- `documents`: `patient_id` (**nullable** [2026-11]), `kind doc_kind`, `title`, `storage_path` (unique), `source document_source`, `uploaded_by` fk profiles, `shared_with_patient` (false), `shared_at`, `archived_at`. Unique `(id, patient_id)`. Check: the path starts with the patient id, or with `unmatched/` when `patient_id` is null. An emailed report is stored with no patient until matched; such a row is visible to **doctors and PAs only**. **Superseded [2026-11, `20261113120000`]:** a row with no patient is read, added and changed by **PAs and admins only**; doctors and nurses no longer see it. Once matched, the patient's in-scope staff read it as before; nurses may add and change rows for in-scope patients. See the release gate below.
- `questions`: `patient_id`, `asked_by` fk profiles, `subject`, `body`, `urgency` ('routine'), `status question_status` ('awaiting_reply'), `ghl_conversation_id`, `answered_at`. Read: patient record level. Insert: someone who can act for the patient. Update (`status`, `answered_at`): team in scope. [2026-11, `20261114120100`] Written by the portal since `feat/finalise-points` (`askQuestion`); status moves only through `reply_to_question` and `close_question` (4.7i), which admit any staff member in scope, health coaches included. **[2026-11, `09969cc`] The update policy "The team changes question status" is dropped and `update` is revoked from `authenticated`** (in `20261114120100`, S-133), so nobody changes `status` or `answered_at` directly (Finding 109). `ghl_conversation_id` is never set (Finding 112).
- `question_replies` [2026-11, `20261114120100`]: `question_id` fk questions, `author_id` fk profiles, `from_clinic` (boolean, not null), `body` (1 to 4,000 characters after trimming), `created_at`. No `updated_at`, no `archived_at`. Indexes on `(question_id, created_at)` and `author_id`. Forced RLS; every privilege revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated`, then select only to `authenticated`; delete and truncate revoked from `service_role`. One policy, select: the question's patient is in `my_patient_ids()` or `in_scope`. **No insert or update for anyone except through `reply_to_question`.** Audit trigger attached.

**Labs and results** (`20261102111711_labs_and_results.sql`)

- `lab_partners`: `name`, `address`, `results_channel`, `api_enabled`. Reference.
- `lab_panels`: `lab_partner_id`, `code` (unique), `name`, `price_pence` (check >= 0), `fasting_required`, `preparation`, `active`, `archived_at`. Reference.
- `markers`: `code` (unique), `name`, `category`, `unit`, `ref_low`, `ref_high`, `explainer`. Reference.
- `lab_panel_markers`: pk `(panel_id, marker_id)`. Reference; admin can insert and delete.
- `lab_orders`: `order_ref` (unique), `patient_id`, `panel_id`, `lab_partner_id`, `ordered_by` fk staff_profiles, `appointment_id`, `due_by` (appointment minus 14 days), `price_pence`, `status order_status` ('sent_to_patient'), `barcode` (unique), `stripe_checkout_id`, `paid_at`, `sample_taken_at`. Read: patient record level. Insert and update: doctor and PA (`ordered_by` must be the writer). Triggers stop a client changing `order_ref`, `patient_id`, `barcode`, `stripe_checkout_id`, `paid_at`, and stop a client setting status to `paid`; the Stripe webhook does that.
- `lab_reports`: `patient_id`, `lab_order_id` (empty for overseas results), `lab_partner_id`, `external_lab_name`, `document_id`, `sample_date`, `received_at`, `status report_status` ('awaiting_review'), `reviewed_by`, `reviewed_at`, `released_at`, `patient_comment`. Check: `status = 'released'` exactly when `released_at` is set. Unique `(id, patient_id)`. **Composite fk `(document_id, patient_id)` to `documents (id, patient_id)`**. Server writes only.
- `result_imports`: `document_id` fk documents, `patient_id` (empty until matched), `lab_report_id` (created on confirm), `status import_status` ('needs_match'), `handled_by`, `received_at`. Read: doctor and PA. Update (`patient_id`, `status`, `handled_by`): doctor and PA, may match or reject but never set `confirmed`; matching is checked against the document's patient. Confirmation is server code. [2026-11, `20261113120000`] Read: a matched import by PAs, doctors, nurses and admins with the patient in scope; an **unmatched** one (`patient_id` null) by **PAs and admins only**. Update: matched imports by in-scope PAs, doctors and nurses, unmatched ones by PAs and admins; `handled_by` must be the writer (or empty). [2026-11, `20261122120000`] Seven nullable columns say what a lab told us: `source_provider` (the short name in the webhook address), `source_report_id` (the lab's id, or a hash of the file), `source_name`, `source_email`, `source_date_of_birth`, `source_sample_date`, `source_test_name`; a check constraint `result_imports_source_length` caps their lengths (60, 200, 200, 254, 200). Unique partial index `result_imports_source_report_idx` on `(source_provider, source_report_id)` where `source_provider is not null`, so a report retried by the lab is recorded once. `source_name` and the other fields are shown to staff to help a match and are never used to find a patient. No policy changed.
- `results`: `lab_report_id`, `patient_id`, `marker_id`, `value_numeric`, `value_text` (check: one of them set), `unit`, `ref_low`, `ref_high`, `flag result_flag`, `doctor_comment`, `original_value` (numeric) and `original_unit` (text) [2026-11, `20261109120000`: the value as the lab reported it, set only when it was converted; both selectable by `authenticated`]. **Composite fk `(lab_report_id, patient_id)` to `lab_reports (id, patient_id)`**. Update (`doctor_comment` only): doctor. Values are locked by trigger.
- `marker_conversions` [2026-11, `20261109120000`]: `marker_id` fk markers, `from_unit` (1 to 20 characters), `factor` (> 0), `offset_value` (default 0), `created_at`; primary key `(marker_id, from_unit)`. Converted value = `value * factor + offset_value`. Select: anyone signed in. Insert and update: admin only; no delete grant. Audit trigger attached. Seeded by the migration and by `seed.sql` with 18 standard factors (testosterone, oestradiol, progesterone, glucose, three cholesterol markers, triglycerides, vitamin D, HbA1c (with offset), B12, ferritin, PSA, CRP, DHEA-S, haemoglobin, free T4, free T3); the clinic's own list replaces them when it arrives.
- `import_items`: `import_id`, `marker_id`, `raw_name`, `value` (text), `unit`, `ref_range_text`, `confidence`, `decision import_decision` ('pending'), `decided_by`. Read: doctor and PA. Update (`marker_id`, `decision`, `decided_by`): doctor and PA, `decided_by` must be the caller. [2026-11, `20261113120000`] Items now follow their import: read by whoever can read the import; updated by a PA, doctor, nurse or admin who can read the import.

**Integrations and compliance** (`20261102111714_integrations_and_compliance.sql`)

- `webhook_events`: `source webhook_source`, `external_event_id`, `event_type`, `payload jsonb`, `status webhook_status` ('received'), `error`, `received_at`, `processed_at`. Unique `(source, external_event_id)` so a payment cannot be handled twice. [2026-11, `20261122120000`] `lab_feed` rows are keyed `<provider>:<report id>`, `event_type` `lab_file`, `status` `processed`, and the payload holds only `provider` and `matched` (no name, email or date of birth).
- `integration_sync_log`: `id bigint identity`, `system sync_system`, `direction`, `entity`, `entity_id`, `external_id`, `status sync_status`, `error`, `started_at`, `finished_at`.
- `audit_log`: `id bigint identity`, `actor_id` fk profiles (empty for system), `actor_role app_role`, `action`, `entity`, `entity_id`, `metadata jsonb`. Append-only (see triggers). Admins read it through `activity_log` (4.7c); clinical staff add `viewed` rows through `log_record_view`.

**Staff calendar** (`20261111120000_google_calendar_connections.sql`) [2026-11, uncommitted, not yet run]

- `google_calendar_connections`: `user_id` (**primary key**, fk profiles, `on delete restrict`; one row per person, no `id` column), `google_email` (3 to 320 characters; stored lower-cased and trimmed by the save function), `refresh_token_encrypted` (1 to 4,000 characters; `v1.<iv>.<tag>.<ciphertext>` from `lib/calendar/crypto.ts`), `scope` (1 to 2,000 characters; what Google granted), `calendar_id` (default `'primary'`, 1 to 320 characters; nothing sets another value), `connected_at` (default now; reset on every reconnect), `updated_at`. **No `created_at`.** RLS enabled and forced. All privileges revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated` and `service_role`, then: `authenticated` may select `user_id`, `google_email`, `calendar_id` and `connected_at` only (never the token or `scope`); `service_role` may select every column and nothing else. One policy, select: the caller's own row, and only while `private.is_active_staff(auth.uid())`. No insert, update or delete for anyone except through the two functions in 4.7d. A disconnect deletes the row rather than archiving it: it is a credential, not a clinical record. `set_updated_at` and audit triggers attached.

- `google_revocations` [2026-11, `20261115120000`]: `id` (uuid), `user_id` fk profiles (`on delete restrict`), `refresh_token_encrypted` (null, or 1 to 4,000 characters; same `v1.` format), `queued_at` (default now), `attempts` (integer, default 0, at least 0), `revoked_at`, `created_at`. No `updated_at`, no `archived_at`, no audit trigger. Indexes: pending by `queued_at` where `revoked_at` is null; `user_id`. RLS enabled and forced, **no policy**. All privileges revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated` and `service_role`, then `service_role` gets `select` and `update (refresh_token_encrypted, attempts, revoked_at)`. Rows are inserted only by the definer trigger `private.drop_leaver_connections()`. The server (`lib/calendar/leavers.ts`) revokes the token at Google and then clears it; nobody signed in can read the queue.

**Clinical notes** (`20261112120300_clinical_notes.sql`) [2026-11]

- `clinical_notes`: `patient_id` fk patient_profiles, `author_id` fk staff_profiles, `appointment_id` fk appointments (nullable; nothing sets it yet), `title` (1 to 200 characters after trimming), `body` (1 to 50,000), `source note_source` ('typed'), `status note_status` ('draft'), `version` (integer >= 1, default 1), `supersedes_id` fk clinical_notes (the signed note this corrects), `signed_at`, `signed_by` fk staff_profiles, `archived_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. Checks: `status = 'signed'` exactly when `signed_at` and `signed_by` are set; a signed note cannot be archived. Indexes on patient (with `created_at desc`), author, signer, appointment, supersedes; **unique `supersedes_id` where not archived**, so one live correction per signed note. Forced RLS. Grants: select to `authenticated` and `service_role`; insert of `patient_id, author_id, appointment_id, title, body, source, supersedes_id` to both; update of `title, body, archived_at` to `authenticated` only; no delete for anyone. Policies: select, anyone with the patient in scope (staff only: **patients and guardians never read notes**; admins and PAs read them); insert, a doctor, nurse or health coach with the patient in scope, `author_id` = own staff id; update, the author, a draft, patient still in scope. Signing only through `sign_clinical_note` (4.7e). Triggers: `clinical_notes_version` (before insert, sets `version` to 1, or one above the superseded note, which must be a signed note of the same patient), `clinical_notes_lock` (before update or delete: no delete ever; a signed or archived note cannot change; patient, author, supersedes, version and source cannot change), `set_updated_at`, audit.
- **Changed [2026-11, `20261113120000`]: a signed note is final.** Nobody corrects it, not its author, not the server: `clinical_note_version` now refuses any insert with `supersedes_id` set (42501, "A signed note is final and cannot be corrected."), checks that a given `appointment_id` belongs to the same patient (22023), and always sets `version` to 1. Insert grants are now `patient_id, author_id, appointment_id, title, body` for `authenticated` and the same plus `source` for `service_role` (`supersedes_id` and, for `authenticated`, `source` are no longer grantable). `supersedes_id`, `version` and the unique index on `supersedes_id` remain for history but nothing can write a correction. The table comment says "Final once signed". `source = 'recording'` notes are written by `recording_transcribed` (4.7g) as drafts in the recorder's name.

- **Changed [2026-11, `20261116120100`, `feat/cqc-audit`]: treatment options.** Five new columns: `all_options_discussed`, `customised_options_discussed`, `nice_options_discussed`, `side_effects_discussed` (each `boolean not null default false`) and `options_offered` (text, null or up to 5,000 characters; not trimmed by the check). `insert` and `update` on all five are granted to `authenticated` (not to `service_role`, so `recording_transcribed` drafts start with all four false and no text). They follow the existing policies and `clinical_notes_lock`: the author changes them on a draft, and nobody changes them once the note is signed. Nothing requires any of them before signing (Finding 139). Column comments describe each one.

**Results sign-off** (`20261116120100_cqc_signoff_and_treatment_options.sql`) [2026-11, `feat/cqc-audit`]

- `lab_report_signoffs`: `report_id` (uuid, **primary key**; no `id`), `patient_id` fk patient_profiles, `outcome` (text, `action` or `no_action`), `action_note` (text, 1 to 2,000 characters after trimming; present exactly when `outcome = 'action'`), `signed_by` fk staff_profiles (not null), `ordered_by` fk staff_profiles (null for a report with no order: copied from `lab_orders.ordered_by` at sign-off), `signed_at` (default now). No `created_at` (against item 3 of 4.14; `signed_at` plays that part), no `updated_at`, no `archived_at`. Composite fk `(report_id, patient_id)` to `lab_reports (id, patient_id)`, so a sign-off always belongs to its report's patient. Every fk `on delete restrict`. Indexes: `(patient_id, signed_at desc)`, `(report_id, patient_id)` (covers the composite fk), `signed_by`, `ordered_by`. Triggers: audit (insert, update, delete) and `lab_report_signoffs_permanent` (4.8). Forced RLS. Grants: all revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated`, then `select` to `authenticated`; `update`, `delete` and `truncate` revoked from `service_role`. One policy, select: "Staff who can open the record read sign-offs", `private.in_scope(patient_id)`. So patients and guardians never read a sign-off (the action note is for the team), and a doctor, nurse or health coach reads it only for a care-team patient (Finding 136). No insert policy or grant: rows are written only by `release_report` (4.7b), one per report. A report released before this migration has no row.

**SignatureRx** (`20261120120000_signaturerx.sql`) [2026-11, `signaturerx`]

- `patient_profiles` gains `address_line1` (text, at most 200), `address_line2` (200), `city` (100) and `postcode` (20), all nullable, under check `patient_profiles_address_length`. Column grants: insert and update to `authenticated` (existing policies decide who). Needed for every SignatureRx prescription.
- `signaturerx_prescribers`: `user_id` (uuid, **primary key**, fk profiles; no `id`), `integration_code` (text, 1 to 200), `created_at`, `updated_at`. Forced RLS and **no policy**; all privileges revoked, then select granted to `service_role` only. Written only by `set_my_signaturerx_code`. Triggers: `set_updated_at`, `write_audit_log`.
- `signaturerx_prescriptions`: `repeat_request_id` fk repeat_requests, `patient_id` fk patient_profiles, `srx_prescription_id` (text, unique, 1 to 100, **nullable**: empty while a send is under way or not confirmed; `confirm_signaturerx_send` fills it in), `action` (text, check `draft` or `issueOnly`), `status` (text, up to 60, null; `sending` and `failed` are Clinic Hub's own, the rest are SignatureRx's), `detailed_status` (60, null), `last_error` (text, up to 300, null; the reason a send was refused, or, after `close_signaturerx_send`, "Closed by hand after checking SignatureRx: " and what was found), `sent_by` fk profiles, `sent_at` (now), `last_checked_at` (null), `created_at`. Indexes on request, patient and sender, and the **partial unique index `signaturerx_prescriptions_one_open_idx`** on `repeat_request_id` where `coalesce(status, '')` is not `failed`, `voided` or `expired`: one open send for each request, and a retry only after one of those. Select: doctors, PAs and admins with the patient in scope (`private.in_scope`). No insert, update or delete policy or grant for `authenticated`; `service_role` has select. Audit trigger. All foreign keys are `ON DELETE RESTRICT`. No medicine, quantity, direction or PIN is held.

**Booking** (`20261113120500_booking.sql`) [2026-11]

- `appointment_types`: `name` (1 to 120 after trimming), `duration_min` (5 to 480), `location_kind location_kind` (clinic or video), `description` (up to 1,000), `bookable_by_patients` (true), `active` (true), `created_at`, `updated_at`. Select: anyone signed in. Insert and update (those six columns): admins. No delete grant. [2026-11, `20261119120000`] New column `bookable_online boolean not null default false` (new patients may book the type on `/book`), with column `insert` and `update` granted to `authenticated` (the admin-only policies still decide). Independent of `bookable_by_patients`.
- `clinician_availability`: `staff_id` fk staff_profiles, `weekday` (smallint 1 = Monday to 7 = Sunday), `start_time`, `end_time` (`time`, London wall-clock; check end after start), `location_id` fk locations (nullable: any location), `active` (true), `created_at`. No `updated_at`. Indexes on `(staff_id, weekday)` and `location_id`. Select: anyone signed in (patients need it for free slots). Insert (`staff_id, weekday, start_time, end_time, location_id, active`): admins, PAs, or the clinician for their own `staff_id`. Update (`start_time, end_time, location_id, active`): the same. No delete: the screen "removes" hours by setting `active` false. **Select changed [2026-11, `29f602f`, edit to `20261113120500`]:** active staff read every clinician's hours; anyone else reads only the hours of clinicians on the current care team of a patient in their `my_patient_ids()` (`private.is_my_clinician`).

Both: forced RLS, every privilege revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated` before the grants, delete and truncate revoked from `service_role`, audit trigger; `set_updated_at` on `appointment_types` only.

**Call recordings** (`20261113120600_call_recordings.sql`) [2026-11]

- `call_recordings`: `patient_id` fk patient_profiles, `recorded_by` fk staff_profiles, `storage_path` (unique; check: its first segment is `patient_id`), `status recording_status` ('uploading'), `provider_job_id` (the AssemblyAI transcript id), `note_id` fk clinical_notes (the draft made from it), `error`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. Indexes on `(patient_id, created_at desc)`, `recorded_by`, `note_id`. Forced RLS; all privileges revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated` **and `service_role`**, then select to `authenticated` and `service_role`, insert of `patient_id, recorded_by, storage_path` to `authenticated`. No update or delete grant to anyone: status changes only through the three functions in 4.7g. Policies: select, staff with the patient in scope (PAs and admins every patient; **patients and guardians never**); insert, a doctor, nurse or health coach with the patient in scope and `recorded_by` = own staff id. `set_updated_at` and audit triggers. Nothing is deleted.

**Education** (`20261112120400_education_and_wearables.sql`) [2026-11]

- `education_courses`: `title` (1 to 200), `description` (up to 4,000), `published` (false), `created_by` fk staff_profiles, `archived_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. Select: clinical staff or admin (the whole library, drafts included), or a patient or guardian through `private.course_is_mine(id)`. Insert: admin, `created_by` = own staff id. Update (`title, description, published, archived_at`): admin.
- `education_sections`: `course_id` fk, `title` (1 to 200), `position` (0), `archived_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. Select: clinical staff or admin, or unarchived sections of a course that is mine. Insert and update (`title, position, archived_at`): admin.
- `education_modules`: `section_id` fk, `title` (1 to 200), `body` (up to 20,000), `video_url` (null or `https://`, up to 2,000), `position` (0), `archived_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. Select: clinical staff or admin, or unarchived modules in an unarchived section of a course that is mine. Insert and update (`title, body, video_url, position, archived_at`): admin.
- `education_assignments`: `course_id` fk, `patient_id` fk, `assigned_by` fk staff_profiles, `archived_at`, `created_at` (no `updated_at`). Unique `(course_id, patient_id)` where not archived. Select: staff with the patient in scope, or the patient and guardians (`my_patient_ids()`, archived rows included). Insert: patient in scope and `assigned_by` = own staff id (so PAs, admins, and doctors, nurses and coaches on the care team). Update (`archived_at` only): patient in scope. [2026-11, `20261114120000`] Select changed: staff in scope as before, but a patient or guardian now reads only **unarchived** assignments of **published, unarchived** courses (Finding 79).

**Education builder** (`20261118120000_education_builder.sql`) [2026-11, `feat/education-builder`, `fc7b5e8`]

- `education_courses.cover_path` (new, nullable): `courses/<course id>/cover-<uuid>.<png|jpg|webp>`, checked so the folder is the row's own id. `update (cover_path)` granted to `authenticated`; the admin-only update policy decides.
- `education_modules.content` (new, jsonb, nullable): an object of at most 200,000 bytes (`pg_column_size`); the lesson document from the editor, cleaned by `cleanLessonDoc` in the server before saving (the database checks only that it is an object). `education_modules.published` (new, boolean, not null, default `true`, so lessons made before the builder count as published; `addLesson` inserts `false`). Insert and update of both granted to `authenticated`. `body` stays and is read only as a fallback for older lessons. The select policy is altered: clinical staff or admin as before; a patient or guardian now reads only lessons that are **published** as well as unarchived, in an unarchived section of a course that is mine.
- `education_files` (new): `module_id` fk education_modules, `storage_path` (unique; `files/<module id>/<uuid>.<pdf|png|jpg>`, folder checked against `module_id`), `file_name` (1 to 200 after trimming), `content_type` (`application/pdf`, `image/png`, `image/jpeg`), `size_bytes` (1 to 20,971,520), `created_by` fk staff_profiles, `archived_at`, `created_at`; no `updated_at`. Indexes on `module_id` (unarchived only) and `created_by`. Forced RLS; everything revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated`; delete and truncate revoked from `service_role`; select, insert of the six content columns and `update (archived_at)` granted to `authenticated`. Policies: select, clinical staff or admin, or an unarchived file of a lesson for which `lesson_is_mine` is true; insert, admin with `created_by` = own staff id and not archived; update, admin. Audit trigger. Archived, never deleted.
- `education_progress` (new): `patient_id` fk patient_profiles, `module_id` fk education_modules, `completed_by` fk profiles, `completed_at` (default now); unique `(patient_id, module_id)`; no `created_at`, `updated_at` or `archived_at`. Indexes on `module_id` and `completed_by`. Forced RLS; everything revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated`; update, delete and truncate revoked from `service_role`; select and insert of `patient_id, module_id, completed_by` granted to `authenticated`, no update or delete grant to anyone. Policies: select, the patient and guardians (`my_patient_ids()`) or staff with the patient in scope; insert, someone who `can_act_for` the patient, `completed_by` = `auth.uid()`, for a published, unarchived lesson in an unarchived section of a published, unarchived course with an unarchived assignment to that patient. Audit trigger. A completion cannot be undone (Finding 163).

**Wearables** (`20261112120400_education_and_wearables.sql`) [2026-11]

- `wearable_readings`: `patient_id` fk, `metric wearable_metric`, `value` (numeric, strictly between -1,000,000 and 1,000,000), `measured_on` (date, from 2000-01-01), `device` (up to 80), `entered_by` fk **profiles**, `archived_at`, `created_at`. Index on `(patient_id, metric, measured_on desc)`. Select: the patient and guardians (unarchived only), or staff with the patient in scope (archived too). Insert: `private.can_act_for(patient_id)` and `entered_by = auth.uid()` (the patient or a guardian who can act; staff cannot enter readings). Update (`archived_at` only): the person who entered it, while they can still act for the patient. Per-metric ranges (`lib/wearables/metrics.ts`) are checked in the server action only, not in the database. **Superseded [2026-11, `20261114120000`]:** trigger `wearable_readings_check` (before insert, `private.check_wearable_reading`, not definer) refuses a `measured_on` after London-time today and a value outside the metric's range (22023); the ranges are a copy of `WEARABLE_METRICS` and must be kept in step by hand.

All five tables: forced RLS, every privilege revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated` before the grants above, delete and truncate revoked from `service_role`, audit trigger; `set_updated_at` on courses, sections and modules.

**Forms and feedback** (`20261117120000_forms_and_feedback.sql`) [2026-11, `feat/patient-feedback`]

- `form_submissions`: `patient_id` fk patient_profiles, `kind form_kind`, `title` (1 to 200 after trimming), `answers` (jsonb: an array of `{"question", "answer"}` in the order asked, 1 to 60 items, at most 64 KB), `source_id` (uuid, **no foreign key**: the repeat request, question or feedback the form made), `submitted_by` fk **profiles**, `submitted_at`, `created_at`. Unique `(kind, source_id)`. Indexes on `(patient_id, submitted_at desc)` and `submitted_by`. No `updated_at`, no `archived_at`: **permanent**. Triggers `form_submissions_permanent` (before update or delete) and `form_submissions_no_truncate` (before truncate) refuse any change for every role, the owner included (42501). Select only, for `authenticated`: the patient and guardians (`my_patient_ids()`, feedback included), staff with the patient `in_scope` for kinds other than feedback, and `private.can_read_feedback` for feedback. No insert for anyone: rows are written by triggers on `repeat_request_items` and `questions`, by `submit_feedback`, and once by the migration's backfill (system actor `forms-backfill`).
- `patient_feedback`: `patient_id` fk patient_profiles, `appointment_id` fk appointments (**unique**, nullable), `sent_at` (now), `reminded_at`, `expires_at` (now + 30 days), `answered_at`, `answered_by` fk profiles, `score` (smallint 0 to 10), `went_well` and `could_improve` (up to 2,000), `wants_contact`, `may_quote` (booleans), `rating` (text, **generated stored**: null until answered, `red` if `wants_contact` or score 0 to 6, `amber` 7 or 8, `green` 9 or 10), `follow_up_due` (date: red the same working day, amber two working days on, null for green; working days skip bank holidays since `20261117120100`), `quote_checked_by` fk **staff_profiles** and `quote_checked_at` ([2026-11, `20261117120100`]: who checked the comments name nobody and hold no health detail before they are used as CQC evidence; check `patient_feedback_quote_checked_whole`, both null or both set; index on `quote_checked_by`; set only by `check_feedback_quote`), `created_at`. Unique `(id, patient_id)` to back the follow-up's composite key. Check: `answered_at`, `answered_by`, `score`, `wants_contact` and `may_quote` are all null or all set. Indexes on `(patient_id, sent_at desc)`, `answered_at desc` (answered only) and `answered_by`. Select: the patient and guardians, or `can_read_feedback`. No insert, update or delete grant to `authenticated`, and insert, update, delete and truncate revoked from `service_role`: written only by the definer functions in 4.7k. Audited; no permanence trigger (the functions update it once).
- `feedback_follow_ups`: `feedback_id` (**primary key**), `id` (unique, checked equal to `feedback_id`, so the audit log, which records a row's `id`, names the feedback), `patient_id`, `note` (1 to 2,000 after trimming), `followed_up_by` fk **staff_profiles**, `followed_up_at`. Composite fk `(feedback_id, patient_id)` to `patient_feedback (id, patient_id)`. No `created_at` (`followed_up_at` instead, against 4.4's opening rule; Finding 150). Indexes on `(feedback_id, patient_id)`, `patient_id`, `followed_up_by`. Permanent, with the same two triggers as `form_submissions`. Select: `can_read_feedback` only (not the patient). Written only by `record_feedback_follow_up`.

All three: RLS enabled and forced, every privilege revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated` before `select` is granted to `authenticated`, insert, update, delete, truncate, references and trigger revoked from `service_role`, audit trigger. Every foreign key `on delete restrict`.

### 4.5 The release gate

Patients and guardians see a result, a report or a lab PDF **only once the report is released**; staff with the patient in scope see everything, released or not. [2026-11] Since `20261112120100` the `results`, `lab_reports` and `documents` select policies are "own and released, or `in_scope`", so a health coach on the care team and every admin also read unreleased data (before, the health coach was held to released data and admins saw none). The gate rests on `lab_reports.released_at` and on composite foreign keys that make a result and a source PDF belong to the same patient as their report. `private.report_is_released(report_id)` guards `results`; `private.document_gate_open(document_id, kind)` guards `documents`: a patient's own upload is always visible, and any file used by an unreleased report stays hidden even if filed under another kind. A document is also visible to a patient only when `shared_with_patient` is true and it is not archived.

### 4.6 Helper functions (schema `private`)

All are `stable`, `SECURITY DEFINER` and `search_path = ''` unless noted; execute is granted to `authenticated` only. `private.auth_throttle_hit` is the exception: it writes and is for the service role only (see 4.15). They are definer so that policies can read `user_roles` and `care_team` without recursing.

| Function | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `is_active_staff(uuid)` | Active, unarchived `staff_profiles` row |
| `has_role(app_role)` | Caller holds the role, is not suspended, and (for staff roles) is active staff |
| `is_clinical_staff()` | doctor, nurse (since `20261112120100` [2026-11]), PA or health coach |
| `is_team()` | doctor, PA or admin |
| `my_patient_ids()` | Own unarchived patient record plus patients of verified guardian links with `can_view` |
| `is_own_patient(uuid)` | The record is the caller's own |
| `can_act_for(uuid)` | Own record, or verified guardian with `can_act` |
| `in_scope(uuid)` | May this staff member open this patient. **Decided [2026-11], `20261112120100`:** false for a null id; true for an admin or a PA; for a doctor, nurse or health coach, true only with a current `care_team` row (`staff_id = my_staff_id()`, `since <= current_date`, `until` null or `> current_date`). Was "any clinical staff member" until then. Every patient-record policy calls it |
| `london_today()` [2026-11, `8399375`, in `20261112120100`] | Today's date in `Europe/London`. Stable, **not** definer; execute to `authenticated` and `service_role`. Used by `in_scope`, `inviter_covers_patient`, the booking functions, `is_my_clinician` and `check_wearable_reading` |
| `is_my_clinician(uuid)` [2026-11, `29f602f`, in `20261113120500`] | The staff member is on the current care team (London dates) of a patient in the caller's `my_patient_ids()`. Used by the `clinician_availability` select policy and `clinician_busy`. Execute: `authenticated` |
| `reject_self_care()`, `care_team_role_matches()`, `drop_leaver_connections()` [2026-11] | Definer trigger functions (`a55db7b`; `20261114120000` for the other two); execute revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated`. See 4.8 |
| `guard_guardian_verifier()`, `check_wearable_reading()` [2026-11, `20261114120000`] | Trigger functions, **not** definer (`search_path = ''`); no explicit revoke (a trigger function cannot be called directly). See 4.8 |
| `is_doctor_only(uuid)` [2026-11, `20261112120200`] | The named person holds `doctor` and neither `pa` nor `admin` (through `person_has_role`). Execute: `service_role` only |
| `inviter_covers_patient(uuid, invitation_kind, uuid)` [2026-11, `20261112120200`] | True unless the kind is `patient` and the inviter is doctor-only and not on that patient's current care team. Used by create, resend and revoke. Execute: `service_role` only |
| `course_is_mine(uuid)` [2026-11, `20261112120400`] | The course is published, not archived, and assigned (unarchived) to a patient in `my_patient_ids()`. Used by the education select policies. Execute: `authenticated` |
| `lesson_is_mine(uuid)` [2026-11, `20261118120000`] | Definer. The lesson is published and not archived, its section is not archived, and `course_is_mine` holds for its course. Used by the `education_files` select policy and `can_read_education_object`. Execute: `authenticated` |
| `can_read_education_object(text)` [2026-11, `20261118120000`] | Definer. True for clinical staff and admins; for anyone else, a `courses/...` object that is the `cover_path` of a course that is mine, or a `files/...` object that is the path of an unarchived `education_files` row whose lesson is mine. Used by the `education` bucket's select policy. Execute: `authenticated` |
| `clinical_note_version()`, `clinical_note_lock()` [2026-11, `20261112120300`] | Trigger functions, **not** definer (`search_path = ''`); execute revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated`. See 4.8 |
| `my_staff_id()` | Caller's `staff_profiles.id`, so a write cannot be attributed to someone else |
| `report_is_released(uuid)`, `document_gate_open(uuid, doc_kind)` | The release gate |
| `storage_object_is_mine(text)` | The caller has already uploaded the file at that path. The documents insert policy requires it of a patient or guardian, so they cannot claim someone else's file or register a row before the file exists [2026-11] |
| `try_uuid(text)` | Cast to uuid or null; `immutable`, not definer |
| `get_my_patient_record()`, `get_staff_directory()` | Definer bodies of the read functions |
| `person_has_role(uuid, app_role)`, `may_invite(uuid, invitation_kind)`, `invitation_lifetime()` [2026-11] | For the invitation functions, which run for the service role and so have no `auth.uid()`: they check a named person with the rules of `has_role` (the role, not suspended, an active staff profile for staff roles). `may_invite`: admins send every kind, PAs patient and guardian, and since `20261112120200` [2026-11] doctors patient invitations (which patients: `inviter_covers_patient`). The lifetime is 3 days. Execute is granted to `service_role`, not `authenticated` |

### 4.7 Read functions (public API)

RLS filters rows, not columns, so patients get hidden-column data through functions. The `SECURITY DEFINER` body lives in `private`; `public.get_my_patient_record()` and `public.get_staff_directory()` are `SECURITY INVOKER` wrappers [2026-11], because the Supabase advisors warn on definer functions in an exposed schema. Execute is granted to `authenticated` only.

- `get_my_patient_record()` returns `id, clinic_ref, first_name, last_name, date_of_birth, sex, contact_email, contact_phone, country, primary_clinician_id, is_self, can_act` for the caller's own record and the records of patients they are a verified guardian of, own record first. No external ids.
- `get_staff_directory()` returns `staff_id, profile_id, full_name, job_title, clinical_role, avatar_path` for the active team. No GMC number, signature or external ids. [2026-11, `20261113120100`] `avatar_path` is returned only when the caller is active staff; patients and guardians get it empty. It replaces the draft's `staff_directory` view and adds `profile_id` so a letter can name its author (`documents.uploaded_by` is a profile id).

The other functions in `public` are not read functions: `auth_throttle_hit` (4.15), the invitation wrappers (4.7a) and the calendar wrappers (4.7d) are for the service role only (except `get_invitable_patients`), while the results functions (4.7b) and the record-view and activity functions (4.7c) are granted to `authenticated` and check the caller inside. [2026-11] Since `feat/clinic-build-2`: the booking functions (4.7f) are granted to `authenticated` except `set_appointment_calendar_event`; the recording functions (4.7g) and `set_patient_ghl_contact` (4.7h) are for the service role only. [2026-11, `feat/finalise-points`] `reply_to_question` and `close_question` (4.7i) are granted to `authenticated`.

### 4.7b Results functions (`20261109120000_results_entry_and_release.sql`) [2026-11, uncommitted]

The tables still give signed-in people no way to insert or change a report or a result. All writes go through these, each a `SECURITY DEFINER` function in `private` (`search_path = ''`) with a `SECURITY INVOKER` wrapper in `public`; execute is granted to `authenticated` (revoked from `public` and `anon`). The checks are inside the function, so the wrapper adds none. Refusals use errcodes 42501, 22023 or 23505 with messages written for people; `lib/results/actions.ts` shows those and hides any other.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `private.result_flag(_value, _low, _high)` (immutable, not definer) | `low` below the low limit, `high` above the high limit, `borderline` inside the range but within 5% of its width (of the limit when only one exists) of an edge, otherwise `normal`. Never returns `critical`: a doctor sets that |
| `register_import(_document_id) returns uuid` | Puts a file in the import queue. Allowed for the patient or an acting guardian on their own `patient_upload`, or a PA or doctor (for a patient in scope, or a file with no patient yet). One queue row per file. Status `needs_match` if the document has no patient, otherwise `matched` |
| `enter_report(_patient, _sample_date, _items jsonb, _lab_name, _document_id, _import_id) returns uuid` | PA or doctor with the patient in scope. Sample date from 1990-01-01 to today; 1 to 80 items of `{marker_id, value, unit}`; no marker twice; the value must be a finite number under 1,000,000,000. A unit other than the marker's needs a row in `marker_conversions` or the call fails; the converted value is rounded to 4 places and `original_value` and `original_unit` keep what was entered. The flag comes from `result_flag` against the marker's reference range, which is copied onto the result. Creates a `lab_reports` row as `awaiting_review`. A document must already be filed under the patient. If an import is named, it becomes `confirmed` with the report and the person who handled it; an import already dealt with fails the whole call |
| [2026-11] Since `20261112120100` | `register_import` and `enter_report` also accept a **nurse** wherever they accept a PA or doctor (the scope rule is still `in_scope`) |
| [2026-11] Since `20261114120000` | `register_import` follows the table policies for a file with **no patient**: only a PA or an admin may queue it (doctors and nurses no longer; Finding 87). A file with a patient: the patient or an acting guardian on their own upload, or a PA, doctor or nurse with the patient in scope |
| `release_report(_report, _comment) returns void` | Doctor only, patient in scope, locks the report. Sets `released`, `reviewed_by`, `reviewed_at`, `released_at` and `patient_comment` (up to 2,000 characters); moves a linked order from `results_in` to `reviewed`; writes a `results` notification to the patient (link `/portal/results`) if they have an account. A second release fails |
| [2026-11, `20261116120100`, `feat/cqc-audit`] `release_report(_report uuid, _comment text default null, _outcome text default null, _action_note text default null) returns void` | **Replaces** the two-argument version, which is dropped (private and public). Same checks and effects as above, plus a sign-off: `_outcome` must be `action` or `no_action` (22023, "Sign the report off: action or no action."); for `action`, `_action_note` must be 1 to 2,000 characters after trimming (22023, "Describe the action, in up to 2,000 characters."). The ordering doctor is read from the linked order (`lab_orders.ordered_by`), null if there is no order. In one transaction it releases the report and inserts the `lab_report_signoffs` row (trimmed action note, or null for `no_action`; `signed_by` = the caller's staff id). Any doctor with the patient in scope may sign off, including one who did not order the tests: it is recorded, not blocked. Execute revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated`, then granted to `authenticated`; the public wrapper is `SECURITY INVOKER` |

### 4.7c Record views and the activity log (`20261110120100_nurse_gender_and_record_views.sql`) [2026-11, uncommitted]

Each is `SECURITY DEFINER` in `private` (`search_path = ''`) with a `SECURITY INVOKER` wrapper in `public`; execute is revoked from `public` and `anon` and granted to `authenticated`. The checks are inside the private function.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `log_record_view(_patient uuid, _entity text, _entity_id uuid default null)` | Returns void. **Since `20261112120100` [2026-11]:** refused (42501, "You cannot open this record.") only if there is no caller or the patient is not `private.in_scope`, so admins, PAs and in-scope nurses, doctors and coaches are all logged; a `patient_profiles` id must equal `_patient`, and a `lab_reports` id is required and must belong to the patient (22023). `actor_role` is still chosen only among doctor, PA and health coach, so a nurse's or an admin's view is written with an empty `actor_role` (Finding 72). Before that: refused unless the caller is clinical staff and the patient in scope. The entity must be `patient_profiles` or `lab_reports` (else 22023), so it cannot put anything else in the log. Writes one `audit_log` row: action `viewed`, the caller's highest-ranked clinical role (doctor, then PA, then health coach), `entity_id` = `_entity_id` or the patient, metadata `{"patient_id": ...}` and nothing else. A repeat by the same actor for the same entity and id within 10 minutes is skipped. **Current [2026-11]:** `actor_role` is the caller's first role in the order doctor, nurse, PA, health coach, admin, so nurses and admins are named (Finding 72 resolved). The entity must still be `patient_profiles` or `lab_reports`: **`documents` is refused (22023)**, which `lib/data/documents.ts` relies on (Finding 105). **Replaced again [2026-11, `09969cc`, in `20261114120100`]:** `documents` is accepted too; like `lab_reports`, it needs an `_entity_id` that is a document of that patient (22023 otherwise) (S-132) |
| `activity_log(_limit integer default 50, _before bigint default null)` | Admin only (42501 otherwise). Returns `id, created_at, actor_name, actor_role, action, entity, patient_name, patient_ref`, newest first, at most 100 rows (the limit is clamped to 1 to 100), keyset paging by `id < _before`. The patient is found from `metadata.patient_id` (through `private.try_uuid`), else from the entity (`patient_profiles`, `lab_reports`, `results`). Names and references only, never clinical values |

### 4.7d Calendar connection functions (`20261111120000_google_calendar_connections.sql`) [2026-11, uncommitted, not yet run]

Each is `SECURITY DEFINER` in `private` (`search_path = ''`) with a `SECURITY INVOKER` wrapper of the same name in `public`. Execute on all four is revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated` and granted to `service_role` only (the migration also repeats `grant usage on schema private to service_role`). Called by server code with `createAdminClient()` after `getCurrentUser()` has identified the person; the user id never comes from a form.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `save_google_calendar_connection(_user uuid, _google_email text, _refresh_token_encrypted text, _scope text) returns void` | Refused (42501, "Only active team members can connect a calendar.") unless `private.is_active_staff(_user)`. Sets `app.actor_id` to `_user`, then inserts or, on conflict on `user_id`, replaces the email, token and scope and resets `connected_at`. It does not check whether the account is suspended; the route does (Finding 64) |
| `remove_google_calendar_connection(_user uuid) returns void` | Sets `app.actor_id` to `_user` and deletes the row. No check on the caller beyond the service-role grant; deleting a missing row is not an error |

### 4.7e Clinical note signing (`20261112120300_clinical_notes.sql`) [2026-11]

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `sign_clinical_note(_note uuid) returns void` | `SECURITY DEFINER` in `private` (`search_path = ''`), `SECURITY INVOKER` wrapper in `public`; execute revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated`, then granted to `authenticated`. Locks the row. Refused (42501, "You cannot sign this note.") unless the note exists, the caller has a staff profile, is its **author** and still has the patient in scope; 23505 if already signed; 22023 if archived. Sets `status = 'signed'`, `signed_at = now()`, `signed_by` = the caller. After this `clinical_note_lock` refuses every change. A doctor cannot sign another person's note, so a transcript draft is signed by its author only |

### 4.7f Booking functions (`20261113120500_booking.sql`) [2026-11]

Each is `SECURITY DEFINER` in `private` (`search_path = ''`) with a `SECURITY INVOKER` wrapper of the same name in `public`. Execute revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated`, then granted as stated. The checks are inside the private function. Times are compared in `Europe/London`.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `clinician_busy(_staff uuid, _from timestamptz, _to timestamptz)` returns `(starts_at, ends_at)` | Stable. The `booked` and `confirmed` appointments of one clinician that overlap the window, **with no patient detail**. Any signed-in caller, any clinician, any window (Finding 92). Execute: `authenticated` |
| `book_appointment(_patient, _staff, _type, _starts_at, _location default null) returns uuid` | The type must be active (22023). The caller must be able to act for the patient (patient or guardian with `can_act`) or have the patient in scope (42501). Booking **as a patient** (can act, not in scope): the type must be `bookable_by_patients` and the clinician on the patient's current care team (London-time `since` and `until`) (42501). The clinician must be an active, unarchived doctor, nurse or health coach (22023). The start must be more than 1 hour and at most 120 days ahead (22023). Start and end must fall on the same London day inside an active `clinician_availability` row for that weekday, matching `_location` when both are set (22023). It then **locks the clinician's `staff_profiles` row** and refuses an overlap with any of their `booked` or `confirmed` appointments (23505, "That time has just been taken."). Inserts the appointment (`status` booked, `source` clinic_hub, `type` = type name, `duration_min` from the type, `booked_by` = caller) and returns its id. Does not check the clinician's Google Calendar: the server's slot list does (section 6). Execute: `authenticated` |
| `cancel_appointment(_appointment uuid)` | Locks the row; refused (42501) unless the caller has the patient in scope, so **patients and guardians cannot cancel**; 22023 unless `booked` or `confirmed`. Sets `status = 'cancelled'`; the row is kept. Any staff role in scope, admins and health coaches included. Execute: `authenticated` |
| `set_appointment_calendar_event(_appointment uuid, _event text)` | Sets `app.system_actor = 'calendar-sync'` and writes `external_id` and `synced_at` on a `clinic_hub` appointment. No comment on the function in the migration. Execute: `service_role` only |
| Changes [2026-11] | `29f602f` (edit to `20261113120500`): `clinician_busy` returns rows only to active staff or for one of the caller's own clinicians (`is_my_clinician`), and only inside now to now plus 121 days (Finding 92 resolved); `book_appointment` refuses a fourth open future booking made as a patient (22023, "You already have three appointments booked…"). `20261114120000` replaces `book_appointment`: a given `_location` must have `kind` equal to the type's `location_kind` (22023, "That location does not suit this appointment."; no location is still allowed for either kind), and the clinician's row is locked `for no key update` instead of `for update` (Finding 113) |
| `get_bookable_clinicians(_patient uuid)` returns `(staff_id, profile_id, full_name, clinical_role)` | Stable. For a caller who can act for the patient: the active doctors, nurses and health coaches on the patient's current care team, by name. Empty for anyone else (staff included). Execute: `authenticated` |

### 4.7g Recording functions (`20261113120600_call_recordings.sql`) [2026-11]

Definer in `private`, invoker wrappers in `public`; execute on all six revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated` and granted to `service_role` only. Called by `lib/recordings/` after the server has checked the person.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `recording_sent(_recording uuid, _job text)` | `app.system_actor = 'transcription'`; sets `status = 'transcribing'`, `provider_job_id`, clears `error`, only from `uploading` or `failed` |
| `recording_failed(_recording uuid, _error text)` | `app.system_actor = 'transcription'`; sets `status = 'failed'` and the first 500 characters of the error, unless already `transcribed` |
| `recording_transcribed(_recording uuid, _title text, _body text) returns uuid` | Locks the row (22023 if missing). Once only: if already `transcribed` it returns the existing `note_id`. Otherwise sets `app.actor_id` to the recorder's user id (so the audit log names the recorder), inserts a **draft** `clinical_notes` row in the recorder's name with `source = 'recording'`, title cut to 200 and body to 50,000 characters (Finding 98), then marks the recording `transcribed` with its `note_id` |

### 4.7h GoHighLevel contact link (`20261113120300_ghl_contact_link.sql`) [2026-11]

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `set_patient_ghl_contact(_patient uuid, _contact text)` | Definer in `private`, invoker wrapper in `public`, execute for `service_role` only. Refuses an empty id or one over 100 characters (22023). Sets `app.system_actor = 'ghl-sync'` and writes `patient_profiles.ghl_contact_id` when it differs. Called by `lib/notify/patient.ts` the first time a patient is messaged through GoHighLevel |

### 4.7i Question functions (`20261114120100_question_replies.sql`) [2026-11]

Definer in `private` (`search_path = ''`), invoker wrappers of the same name in `public`; execute on all four revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated`, then granted to `authenticated`. The checks are inside.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `reply_to_question(_question uuid, _body text) returns uuid` | Locks the question (22023 if missing). The caller replies **as the clinic** if the patient is `in_scope` (any staff role: PA, admin, doctor, nurse, health coach), otherwise **as the patient side** if they can act for the patient (patient or guardian with `can_act`), else 42501. 22023 if the question is `closed` or the body is not 1 to 4,000 characters after trimming. Inserts the reply (`author_id` = caller, `from_clinic` = in scope, body trimmed); then a clinic reply sets `status = 'answered'` and `answered_at = now()`, a patient-side reply sets `status = 'awaiting_reply'`. **Returns `boolean`** [2026-11, `09969cc`]: true when the reply is from the clinic (it returned the reply id before), so the server learns the side from the database (S-130) |
