| `close_question(_question uuid) returns void` | Locks the question; 42501 unless it exists and the patient is `in_scope`. Sets `status = 'closed'`. Closed stays closed: no function reopens it |

### 4.7j Repeat prescription functions (`20261116120000_repeat_prescriptions.sql`) [2026-11, `feat/repeat-prescriptions`]

Definer in `private` (`search_path = ''`), with invoker wrappers of the same name in `public` except `is_weight_loss_name` (private only). Execute is revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated` on all eleven; then `private.is_weight_loss_name` and both copies of `request_repeat`, `cancel_repeat_request` and `decide_repeat_request` are granted to `authenticated`, and both copies of `mark_repeat_request_paid` and `mark_run_out_reminded` to `service_role` only.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `private.is_weight_loss_name(_name text) returns boolean` (stable) | True if the name contains, ignoring case, the first word of an unarchived `prescribable_medicines` row marked `weight_loss` ("Mounjaro 5 mg" matches "Mounjaro"). Mirrored by `isWeightLossName` in `lib/prescriptions/constants.ts` for the form |
| `request_repeat(_patient uuid, _items jsonb, _has_future_appointment boolean, _wants_appointment boolean, _still_losing_weight boolean, _weight_lost_last_month text, _has_side_effects boolean, _side_effects text, _note text) returns uuid` | 42501 unless the caller can act for the patient (`can_act_for`) or is a PA with the patient in scope. 22023 for a missing appointment answer, not 1 to 10 items, or an answer over its length. Every item is checked before anything is written: `supply_months` 1 or 2; exactly one of `medication_id` (an active, unarchived medicine of this patient, not repeated), `medicine_id` (an unarchived list entry, not repeated) or `other_name` (up to 200). If any item is a weight-loss medicine, `_still_losing_weight` and `_has_side_effects` are required, and side effects must be described when yes. Inserts the request (`requested_by` the caller, `fee_pence` its default 3000; `wants_appointment` kept only without a future appointment; weight-loss answers kept only when a weight-loss medicine is present) and its items, and returns the id. Messages are written for people and passed on by the action |
| `cancel_repeat_request(_request uuid) returns void` | Locks the row. 42501 unless it exists and the caller can act for the patient, or is a PA or doctor with the patient in scope (admins removed by the review fixes [2026-11]; the first commit also admitted them). 22023 unless unpaid, `requested` and not already withdrawn. Sets `cancelled_at` |
| `decide_repeat_request(_request uuid, _status request_status, _note text) returns void` | Locks the row. 42501 unless the caller is a PA or doctor with the patient in scope (admins removed [2026-11], as for withdrawal). 22023 if withdrawn, for a note over 2,000, or a decline without a note. Allowed moves: `requested` to `approved`, `declined` or `sent_to_pharmacy`; `approved` to `declined` or `sent_to_pharmacy`. Sets `status`, `decided_by` (the caller), `decision_note` (the new note, or the old one kept when blank) and `resolved_at` (now for declined or sent, else null). **Payment is not checked** (Finding 122) |
| `mark_repeat_request_paid(_request uuid, _session text, _payment_intent text, _amount integer) returns boolean` | Service role only. Names system actor `stripe` for the audit row. Locks the row; false if missing or already paid, so a repeated webhook does nothing. 22023 if `_amount` is not `fee_pence`. Sets `paid_at`, `stripe_checkout_session_id` and `stripe_payment_intent_id`, clears `cancelled_at` (a late payment reinstates a withdrawn request) and returns true. Status is not checked |
| `mark_run_out_reminded(_medication uuid, _run_out date) returns void` | Service role only. System actor `prescription-reminder`; sets `medications.run_out_reminded_for` |

### 4.7k Forms and feedback functions (`20261117120000_forms_and_feedback.sql`) [2026-11, `feat/patient-feedback`]

All in `private` with `search_path = ''`. Execute is revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated` on all of them; then `can_read_feedback` (used by the policies), and both copies of `submit_feedback` and `record_feedback_follow_up`, are granted to `authenticated`; both copies of `queue_feedback` and `due_feedback_reminders` to `service_role` only. The four `public` wrappers are `security invoker`.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `private.reject_permanent_change()` (trigger, not definer) | Raises 42501 "This record is permanent and cannot be changed or removed." Used on `form_submissions` and `feedback_follow_ups` |
| `private.working_day_from(_day date) returns date` (immutable) | The first Monday-to-Friday day on or after the date. Bank holidays were not counted (Finding 147); **replaced [2026-11] by `20261117120100`** to skip bank holidays too (4.7l) |
| `private.can_read_feedback(_patient uuid) returns boolean` (stable, definer) | `in_scope(_patient)` and the caller is admin, PA or doctor. So doctors on the care team, every PA and admin; never a nurse or health coach |
| `private.form_line(_question, _answer) returns jsonb`, `private.yes_no(boolean) returns text` (immutable) | One answer line, or null when the answer is blank; "Yes", "No" or null |
| `private.repeat_request_answers(_request uuid)`, `private.question_answers(_question uuid)` (stable, definer) `returns jsonb` | The form as asked: the medicines (one line each with one or two months), the Typeform questions and the note; or the subject, urgency and question. Blank answers are left out |
| `private.record_repeat_request_form()`, `private.record_question_form()` (triggers, definer) | Copy a repeat request (statement trigger after insert on `repeat_request_items`, so the medicines are in) or a question (row trigger after insert on `questions`) to `form_submissions`; `on conflict (kind, source_id) do nothing` |
| `queue_feedback() returns table (feedback_id uuid, patient_id uuid)` (definer) | Service role. System actor `feedback`. For each patient, the latest appointment that **started** one to three London days ago and is `completed`, or (**uncommitted working-tree change [2026-11]**) `booked` or `confirmed` and already ended (`starts_at + duration_min` before now), because nothing marks a visit completed yet; cancelled and no-show visits are left out. Only then, if the patient is unarchived, has an unsuspended account or a guardian who can act with one, the appointment has no survey, and no survey was sent to the patient in the last 90 days: inserts a `patient_feedback` row and returns it |
| `due_feedback_reminders() returns table (feedback_id uuid, patient_id uuid)` (definer) | Service role. System actor `feedback`. Sets `reminded_at` on unanswered, unreminded, unexpired surveys sent three or more days ago and returns them |
| `submit_feedback(_feedback uuid, _score integer, _went_well text, _could_improve text, _wants_contact boolean, _may_quote boolean) returns text` (definer) | Locks the row. 42501 unless the caller can act for the patient (`can_act_for`); 23505 if already answered; 22023 if closed, the score is missing or outside 0 to 10, either yes-or-no is missing, or a text is over 2,000. Stores the answer (texts trimmed, blank as null) and `follow_up_due`, writes the `form_submissions` copy, and for amber or red inserts a `messages` notification linking to `/clinic/feedback/<id>` for every unsuspended admin and PA, and for red also the patient's current care-team doctors; never to the person answering, the patient, or the patient's guardians. Returns `green`, `amber` or `red` |
| `record_feedback_follow_up(_feedback uuid, _note text) returns void` (definer) | 42501 unless the caller has a staff profile and `can_read_feedback`; 22023 if unanswered or the note is blank or over 2,000; 23505 if a follow-up exists. Inserts the permanent row. Green feedback may be followed up too |

### 4.7l Feedback follow-up fixes (`20261117120100_feedback_follow_up_fixes.sql`) [2026-11, `feat/patient-feedback`, `2171c59`]

All in `private` with `search_path = ''`. Execute is revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated` on all of them, then granted to `authenticated` on both copies of `check_feedback_quote` and `log_feedback_export` only. The two `public` wrappers are `security invoker` SQL functions.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `private.easter_sunday(_year integer) returns date` (immutable) | Easter Sunday by the anonymous Gregorian algorithm |
| `private.is_bank_holiday(_day date) returns boolean` (immutable) | The regular bank holidays in England and Wales: New Year's Day, Good Friday, Easter Monday, the first and last Mondays in May, the last Monday in August, Christmas Day and Boxing Day, with substitute weekdays when one falls at a weekend. One-off holidays (a coronation, a jubilee) are not included (Finding 154) |
| `private.working_day_from(_day date) returns date` (immutable, `create or replace`) | The first day on or after the date that is not a Saturday, Sunday or bank holiday. Used by `submit_feedback` for `follow_up_due` |
| `check_feedback_quote(_feedback uuid, _checked boolean) returns void` (definer) | Locks the row. 42501 "You cannot check this feedback." unless the caller has a staff profile and `can_read_feedback`; 22023 if `_checked` is null, or if checking an answer that is unanswered, not `may_quote`, or has neither comment. Sets `quote_checked_by` and `quote_checked_at` to the caller and now, or clears both. Does not require a green rating (Finding 156). Audited by the table's audit trigger |
| `log_feedback_export(_set text, _rows integer) returns void` (definer) | 42501 unless signed in and admin, PA or doctor; 22023 unless `_set` is `evidence` or `all` and `_rows` is 0 or more. Inserts one `audit_log` row: the caller, their highest staff role (doctor, then PA, then admin), action `exported`, entity `patient_feedback`, no entity id, metadata `{set, rows}`. The row count is whatever the caller passes (Finding 155) |

### 4.7m Course reordering (`20261118120000_education_builder.sql`) [2026-11, `feat/education-builder`, `fc7b5e8`]

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `reorder_course(_course uuid, _order jsonb) returns void` (definer, plpgsql) | 42501 "Only an admin can change a course." unless the caller is an admin. `_order` is `[{"section": uuid, "lessons": [uuid, ...]}, ...]`, at most 200 entries; 22023 if it is not an array, a section id or lesson id is not a UUID, or `lessons` is not an array. 40001 "The course has changed. Reload the page and try again." unless the order names every unarchived section of the course and every unarchived lesson in those sections exactly once and nothing else. Then sets each section's `position` and each lesson's `position` and `section_id` (so a lesson can move to another section of the same course), writing only rows that change. Takes no row locks. The `public` copy is a `security invoker` SQL wrapper. Execute on both: `authenticated` only. [2026-11, `20261120120300_reorder_course_lint.sql`] The private function is replaced whole to start two `uuid[]` variables from `array[]::uuid[]` instead of the text `'{}'`, which the database linter flagged; the behaviour is unchanged. Not applied on the hosted project |

### 4.7n Diary and online booking functions (`20261119120000_clinic_diary_and_online_booking.sql`) [2026-11, `feat/clinic-diary`, `4f3c29c`]

Each is `SECURITY DEFINER` in `private` (`search_path = ''`) with a `SECURITY INVOKER` SQL wrapper of the same name in `public`. Execute revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated` on all ten, then granted as stated. Hours are compared in `Europe/London`.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `clinic_diary(_from timestamptz, _to timestamptz)` returns `(id, starts_at, duration_min, type, status, clinician_id, location_id, patient_id, patient_name, clinic_ref, can_open, booked_online, new_patient)` | Stable, plpgsql. 42501 "Only the team reads the diary." unless the caller has a staff profile and is doctor, nurse, PA, health coach or admin. 22023 "Choose up to six weeks at a time." if either bound is null, `_to <= _from` or the range is over 43 days. Every appointment of **every patient** overlapping the range, except `cancelled` (archived patients included), with the patient's full name and clinic reference whatever the care team; `can_open` is `private.in_scope(patient_id)`; `new_patient` is `booked_online_at` set and no `user_id`. Ordered by start and clinician. [2026-11, `49cb8b8`] Also returns `source appt_source` as the last column. **Replaced by `20261121120000` [2026-11] (4.7p):** a doctor, nurse or health coach now gets only their own bookings. Execute: `authenticated` |
| `move_appointment(_appointment uuid, _staff uuid, _starts_at timestamptz, _location uuid default null) returns void` | Locks the appointment. 42501 "You cannot move this appointment." unless it exists, the caller has the patient in scope and is admin, PA, doctor, nurse or health coach. 22023 unless `booked` or `confirmed`; unless `source = 'clinic_hub'` ("…booked in another system. Move it there."); unless `_staff` is an active, unarchived doctor, nurse or health coach; unless `_starts_at` is in the future and within 366 days; if the appointment has a type, unless `_location` (when given) is of the type's `location_kind`; unless the whole appointment fits one active hours row of `_staff` on that London weekday (location null, or the row's location null or equal). Locks the clinician `for no key update`, then 23505 if it overlaps another `booked` or `confirmed` appointment of `_staff`. Sets `clinician_id`, `starts_at`, `location_id = _location` and `status = 'booked'`. Does not require the clinician to be on the patient's care team, nor the type to be active. Audited through the `appointments` trigger. **Changed by `49cb8b8` [2026-11]:** also 42501 if the patient is archived; the clinician's profile must be `active`; the location is `_location` if given, else the matching hours row's location (rows with a location first), and is then checked against the type's kind (22023); the status is **kept** (a `confirmed` booking stays confirmed). Still no care-team or active-type check. Execute: `authenticated` |
| `online_booking_clinicians()` returns `(staff_id, profile_id, full_name, job_title)` | Stable, SQL. Active, unarchived doctors, nurses and health coaches whose profile is `active` and who have at least one active hours row, by name. Not filtered by appointment type. Execute: `service_role` only |
| `online_busy(_staff uuid, _from timestamptz, _to timestamptz)` returns `(starts_at, ends_at)` | Stable, SQL. The `booked` and `confirmed` appointments of one clinician overlapping the window, clamped to now to now plus 121 days, with no patient detail. Execute: `service_role` only |
| `book_online(_type, _staff, _starts_at, _location, _first_name, _last_name, _date_of_birth, _email, _phone)` returns `(appointment_id uuid, patient_id uuid)` | plpgsql. Sets `app.system_actor = 'online-booking'` for the transaction (cleared at the end). 22023 with a patient-facing message unless: the type is active and `bookable_online`; both names are non-blank and at most 80 characters; date of birth from 1900 to today **and at least 18 years ago** ("Online booking is for adults…"); the email (trimmed, lower-cased) matches a simple pattern and is at most 254 characters; the phone matches `^\+?[0-9 ()-]{7,20}$`; fewer than **two** open future `booked_online` appointments already exist for patients with that `contact_email`; the clinician is an active, unarchived doctor, nurse or health coach; the start is more than 24 hours and at most 120 days ahead; a given location suits the type's kind; the appointment fits one active hours row. Locks the clinician `for no key update`, then 23505 "That time has just been taken…" on an overlap. Then inserts a **new** `patient_profiles` row (next `SC-` reference from `private.patient_ref_seq`, trimmed names, lower-cased email, `booked_online_at = now()`, no `user_id`, no care team), a `clinic_hub` appointment with the type's name and length and `booked_online = true` (`booked_by` null), and one `appointments` notification ("New online booking", no name, link `/clinic/diary/<id>`) for every admin and PA whose profile is not suspended. **Changed by `49cb8b8` [2026-11]:** the limit is two open future online bookings per email **or per phone number (digits compared)**, or 20 online bookings created in the last hour clinic-wide, all with one message, "We cannot take this booking online. Please contact the clinic."; the window is more than 24 hours and at most **25 days** ahead ("Choose a time at least a day ahead, within the next three weeks."); the adult check uses `private.london_today()`; the clinician's profile must be `active`; the booked clinician is inserted into `care_team` (relationship `doctor`, `nurse` or `health_coach` by clinical role, `since` London today); only admins and PAs whose profile is `active` are notified. **Replaced by `20261121120000` [2026-11] (4.7p):** an existing patient is booked on their own record. Execute: `service_role` only |

### 4.7o SignatureRx functions (`20261120120000_signaturerx.sql`) [2026-11, `signaturerx`]

Definer in `private` (`search_path = ''`) with invoker wrappers of the same name in `public`. Execute is revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated` on all, then granted as below. `record_signaturerx_prescription` no longer exists (replaced by the open-then-close pair). `staff_may_send_prescription` has no public wrapper.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `set_my_signaturerx_code(_code text) returns void` | `authenticated`. 42501 unless the caller is a doctor; 22023 unless the trimmed code is 1 to 200 characters. Inserts or replaces the caller's own row |
| `has_my_signaturerx_code() returns boolean` (stable) | `authenticated`. Whether the caller has a saved code |
| `private.staff_may_send_prescription(_by uuid, _patient uuid) returns boolean` (stable) | `service_role` only; no public wrapper. True when `_by` holds the doctor role, is active staff, is on the patient's care team today (`since` to `until`, London date), and is neither the patient nor one of their guardians. A doctor can never prescribe for themselves. Mirrors `private.in_scope` for a named person |
| `can_send_to_signaturerx(_request uuid) returns boolean` (stable) | `authenticated` (private function and public wrapper). True when the request is `approved`, not withdrawn, and `staff_may_send_prescription` holds for the signed-in user and the request's patient |
| `start_signaturerx_send(_request uuid, _action text, _by uuid) returns uuid` | `service_role` only. Names `_by` as the audit actor. Locks the request. 22023 unless it is `approved` and not withdrawn; 42501 unless `staff_may_send_prescription`; 23505 (one open send for each request) if one is open. Inserts a `sending` row and returns its id |
| `finish_signaturerx_send(_id uuid, _srx_id text, _status text, _detailed text) returns void` | `service_role` only. System actor `signaturerx`. Sets the SignatureRx id, statuses and `last_checked_at` on a `sending` row; 22023 "That send is not open." otherwise |
| `fail_signaturerx_send(_id uuid, _reason text) returns void` | `service_role` only. System actor `signaturerx`. Sets a `sending` row to `failed` with `last_error` (first 300 characters), so another try is allowed. No error if the row is not open |
| `close_signaturerx_send(_id uuid, _reason text) returns void` | [2026-11, `20261120120200`; applied on the hosted project by the owner under its old name `20261118120000`] `authenticated` (private definer function and invoker public wrapper). Locks the row. 22023 unless it is `sending`; 42501 unless the caller is an admin or `staff_may_send_prescription((select auth.uid()), patient)` holds; 22023 unless the trimmed reason is 1 to 200 characters. Sets `status = 'failed'` and `last_error` to "Closed by hand after checking SignatureRx: " plus the reason, so the request can be sent again |
| `confirm_signaturerx_send(_id uuid, _srx_id text) returns void` | [2026-11, `20261120120200`; applied on the hosted project under its old name] `authenticated`, same gate as above. 22023 unless the row is `sending`; 22023 unless the trimmed id is 1 to 100 characters; 23505 when the id is already recorded for another row. Sets `srx_prescription_id` and clears `status`, `detailed_status` and `last_checked_at`, so the next check with SignatureRx fills the state in. The `status` is then null, which the partial unique index still counts as open. No audit call in the function itself; the table's audit trigger records the update (the actor is the caller) |
| `refresh_signaturerx_prescription(_id uuid, _status text, _detailed text) returns void` | `service_role` only. System actor `signaturerx`; sets the two statuses and `last_checked_at` on a row that has a SignatureRx id and is not `failed` |

### 4.7p Diary access and one record per person (`20261121120000_diary_access_and_one_record.sql`) [2026-11, `feat/diary-access`, `1309215`]

`create or replace` of two functions from 4.7n, same signatures, return types, `SECURITY DEFINER`, `search_path = ''` and grants (the migration does not repeat any grant or the `public` wrappers, which are unchanged). The migration header records the clinic's decisions of 12 November 2026, including that bookings already in GoHighLevel calendars stay there and nothing is imported.

| Function | What changed |
|---|---|
| `clinic_diary(_from, _to)` | Same checks and columns. Rows are now limited to `private.has_role('admin') or private.has_role('pa') or a.clinician_id = private.my_staff_id()`: admins and PAs read every clinician's bookings (every patient, names included, archived patients included); a doctor, nurse or health coach reads only bookings where they are the clinician, whatever the care team. A team member with both a clinical role and `pa` or `admin` sees everything. |
| `book_online(...)` | Same validation, limits, hours and overlap checks as 4.7n. Before them it looks for unarchived `patient_profiles` rows with the same `date_of_birth` whose `contact_email` **or** the account's `profiles.email` (left join on `user_id`) equals the trimmed, lower-cased email. One match: that record is used. More than one: the one whose trimmed first name matches case-insensitively, else 22023 "We cannot take this booking online. Please contact the clinic." (the same message as the misuse limits). None: a new record is inserted as before. An existing record is never changed (names, phone and email typed are ignored; `booked_online_at` is not set). The open-bookings limit also counts the matched record's own online bookings. The clinician is added to `care_team` only if no current row (`since <= london_today()` and `until` null or later) exists for them. The admin and PA notice body is "An existing patient has booked online, on their own record." or, for a new record, "A new patient has booked online. Check their details and invite them to the portal." Because a second online booking by the same person matches the first online record's `contact_email` and date of birth, it reuses it. |

`move_appointment` was also changed in place in `20261119120000` by `e79f447` [2026-11]: after the move, the clinician the booking moves to is added to the patient's `care_team` (relationship by clinical role, `since` London today) if not already current on it.

### 4.7q Lab webhook functions (`20261122120000_lab_webhook.sql`) [2026-11, `feat/lab-webhook`, uncommitted]

Adds `lab_feed` to `webhook_source` and `document_source` (`alter type ... add value if not exists`), the `result_imports` source columns and unique index (see 4.4), and three functions, each a `private` `SECURITY DEFINER` function with `search_path = ''` and a `public` `SECURITY INVOKER` SQL wrapper, as elsewhere. All grants are revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated` first.

| Function | Who can run it | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `find_patient_for_lab(_email, _dob)` returns `uuid` | `service_role` only | `stable`. Lower-cases and trims the email; returns null when the email is empty or the date of birth is null. Looks for unarchived `patient_profiles` with that `date_of_birth` whose `contact_email` **or** the account's `profiles.email` equals the email. Returns the id only when exactly one distinct patient matches, otherwise null, and never says whether the email or the date was the miss. |
| `receive_lab_file(_provider, _external_id, _patient, _storage_path, _title, _name, _email, _dob, _sample_date, _test_name)` returns `(import_id uuid, is_new boolean)` | `service_role` only | Sets `app.system_actor = 'lab-webhook'` for the transaction and resets it. Refuses a provider not matching `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,40}$` or an id that is empty or over 200 characters (`22023`). Inserts a `webhook_events` row (`on conflict do nothing`); if it already existed, returns the existing import with `is_new` false. Otherwise inserts a `documents` row (`kind lab_report`, `source lab_feed`, `shared_with_patient` false, `patient_id` possibly null, title cut to 120) and a `result_imports` row: `matched` with the patient when one was found, else `needs_match`, with the source columns. The path rules are enforced by existing document constraints (the test covers a path not starting with the patient id, or `unmatched/` for a matched file). |
| `match_import_to_patient(_import, _patient, _new_path)` returns void | `authenticated`, but refused (`42501`) unless the caller has the `pa` or `admin` role, checked inside | Locks the import. It must be `needs_match` with no patient (else `22023`, "not waiting for a match"); the patient must exist and be unarchived; `_new_path` must start with the patient id. Then sets the document's `patient_id` and `storage_path`, and the import's `patient_id` and `status = matched`. Server code has already moved the file in storage. |

`supabase/tests/25_lab_webhook.test.sql` (`no_plan`, not run by this document): the patient lookup (case and spaces, wrong date, unknown or missing email or date, the sign-in email, two patients with the same details match nobody, an archived record is left out); `receive_lab_file` (a matched and a waiting file, the same report twice is recorded once and returns the same import, bad provider and id are refused, no name, email or date of birth in the stored event, `lab_feed` source, not shared with the patient); `match_import_to_patient` (a doctor and a patient are refused, a matched file or an unknown patient or a wrong path is refused, a PA matches once and not twice, the document moves); and the grants (`authenticated` and `anon` cannot call the other two functions or match).


### 4.7a Invitation functions (`20261105120000_invitations.sql`) [2026-11, uncommitted]

**Changed by `20261114120000_finalise_hardening.sql` [2026-11]:** `create_invitation` and `accept_invitation` were replaced (same signatures). The only changes: a guardian invitation is refused as "already a guardian" only while a link with `can_view` or `can_act` exists, and accepting a guardian invitation reactivates a removed link instead of failing on the unique key (Finding 88).

**Changed by `20261112120200_doctors_invite_patients.sql` [2026-11]:** `create_invitation`, `resend_invitation`, `revoke_invitation` and `get_invitable_patients` were replaced (same signatures). A doctor (not PA or admin) may now send a patient invitation, but only for a patient on their current care team, or a **new** patient, whom the function puts on the doctor's care team as `primary_doctor` in the same transaction. Otherwise 42501 "You can invite only your own patients." Resend and revoke also require `inviter_covers_patient`, so a doctor resends or cancels only patient invitations for their own patients. `get_invitable_patients` returns, for a PA or a doctor, the unarchived patients in `in_scope` (a doctor's care-team patients; a PA's every patient), and for an admin all. The admin-notice and PA-notice rules are unchanged; the email to Dr Gina Schoeman on a doctor or admin invitation is sent by `sendInvitation`, not by the database (section 6).

Each is `SECURITY DEFINER` with `search_path = ''` in `private`, with a `SECURITY INVOKER` wrapper of the same name in `public` that is granted to `service_role` only (verified: `anon`, patient, doctor, health coach, PA and admin are refused, 401 or 403). The one exception is `get_invitable_patients`, also granted to `authenticated`. Every writer first sets `app.actor_id` to the inviter, so the audit log names the right person.

| Function | What it does |
|---|---|
| `create_invitation(_inviter, _kind, _email, _token_hash, _full_name, _role, _patient_id, _relationship, _can_act, _job_title, _new_patient jsonb, _confirm_email_mismatch boolean default false)` (replaced in `20261105130000`; the private function names the last argument `_confirm_mismatch`) | `may_invite` by name; refuses a self-invite, an invalid address, a team invitation to someone with a staff profile, a patient who already has an account or whose address belongs to another patient record, a guardian who is the patient or already a guardian. Cancels expired pending invitations to the address. With `_new_patient` it first inserts a minimal `patient_profiles` row (names, date of birth, `contact_email` for patient invitations) with the clinic reference `SC-` plus a number from `private.patient_ref_seq` (starts at 100000). One pending invitation per address (a duplicate gets a plain message). **Since `20261105130000` [2026-11]:** a patient invitation for an existing record whose `contact_email` differs from the address is refused (errcode 22023, hint `email_mismatch`) unless `_confirm_email_mismatch` is true; one live patient invitation per record; and notices are written to `notifications` (topic `messages`, link `/clinic/team`) without blocking the invitation: a doctor or admin invitation notifies the other admins, a guardian invitation with `_can_act` notifies the other PAs. Returns the invitation id |
| `resend_invitation(_inviter, _invitation, _token_hash)` | `may_invite` for the old row's kind; cancels the old row and every other unused invitation to the address, and makes a new row with a new hash and a new 3-day expiry |
| `revoke_invitation(_inviter, _invitation)` | `may_invite`; sets `revoked_at`; refuses an accepted one |
| `get_invitation(_token_hash)` | Read only. Returns `status` (`valid`, `used`, `cancelled`, `expired`, `not_found`), kind, role, email, name, job title, the inviter's name, whether an account exists for the address, and the expiry. A deactivated, suspended or no-longer-allowed inviter reads as `cancelled` |
| `accept_invitation(_token_hash, _user_id, _full_name, _date_of_birth date default null)` | One transaction, row locked: unused, not cancelled, not expired; the inviter may still send that kind; the account's email equals the invited address (case-insensitively) and it is not suspended. **Patient and guardian invitations also need `_date_of_birth` to equal the patient record's date of birth, or it raises "The date of birth does not match."** (team invitations need none) [2026-11]. Team: adds the staff profile (`clinical_role` from the role, active) and the role. Patient: sets `patient_profiles.user_id` (only if empty and not archived) and adds the `patient` role. Guardian: adds the `patient_guardians` link (`can_view`, `can_act` as sent, `verified_by` the inviter's staff profile). Then activates the profile, fills a blank name, marks the invitation accepted and notifies the inviter. Returns the kind |
| `verify_invitation_identity(_token_hash, _date_of_birth date default null)` [2026-11] | Returns text, never raises for a wrong date, so the count is kept. `invalid` (unknown, used, cancelled or expired), `ok` (team invitation, or the date matches), `wrong` (adds 1 to `failed_attempts`) or `locked` (the fifth wrong date sets `revoked_at` and notifies the inviter "An invitation was cancelled"). Runs with `app.system_actor = 'invitation-identity-check'` for the audit trigger. Service role only |
| `get_invitable_patients()` | For signed-in PAs (patients in `in_scope`) and admins (all): `id`, first and last name, `clinic_ref`, `has_account` for unarchived patients. Nothing clinical. An empty list for everyone else |

### 4.8 Triggers

| Trigger | Function | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `*_set_updated_at` on `profiles`, `notification_preferences`, `patient_profiles`, and `google_calendar_connections` [2026-11] | `private.set_updated_at` | Keeps `updated_at` |
| `on_auth_user_created` on `auth.users` | `private.handle_new_user` (definer) | Creates the `profiles` row; grants no role |
| `on_auth_user_email_changed` on `auth.users` (after update of `email`, only when it changes) [2026-11, uncommitted] | `private.sync_profile_email` (definer) | Copies the confirmed sign-in email to `profiles.email`, so the two never drift. A person cannot change `profiles.email` themselves (the trigger below and the column grant) |
| `profiles_protect_columns` | `private.protect_profile_columns` | Client cannot change `id` or `email`; only an admin changes `status`; an admin cannot edit another person's details |
| `staff_profiles_protect_columns` | `private.protect_staff_columns` | Non-admins cannot change title, role, GMC number, `active`, `archived_at` |
| `*_reject_client_changes` on `staff_profiles`, `patient_profiles`, `medications`, `repeat_requests`, `documents`, `questions`, `lab_orders`, `result_imports`, `results` | `private.reject_client_changes(cols...)` | A second lock behind the column grants: `current_user = 'authenticated'` cannot change the named columns; service role and migrations are unaffected |
| `lab_orders_reject_client_paid_status` | `private.reject_client_paid_status` | A client cannot set an order to `paid` |
| `<table>_write_audit_log` on 27 tables in the audit migration, plus `marker_conversions` (`20261109120000`) and `google_calendar_connections` (`20261111120000`) [2026-11] | `private.write_audit_log` (definer) | Audits insert, update and delete. For the calendar table this records connecting, reconnecting and disconnecting under the staff member; changes to Google events are not audited |
| `audit_log_reject_update_delete`, `audit_log_reject_truncate` | `private.reject_audit_change` | `audit_log` is append-only, even for the service role |
| [2026-11] `*_write_audit_log` on `clinical_notes`, `education_courses`, `education_sections`, `education_modules`, `education_assignments`, `wearable_readings`; `*_set_updated_at` on `clinical_notes` and the three education content tables | `private.write_audit_log`, `private.set_updated_at` | As above |
| [2026-11, `20261118120000`] `education_files_write_audit_log`, `education_progress_write_audit_log` | `private.write_audit_log` | As above. Neither table has a `set_updated_at` trigger (no `updated_at` column) |
| [2026-11] `clinical_notes_version` (before insert) | `private.clinical_note_version` | Version 1 for a new note; for a correction, one above the superseded note, which must be signed and belong to the same patient (22023 otherwise) |
| [2026-11] `clinical_notes_lock` (before update or delete) | `private.clinical_note_lock` | For every role, the service role and the owner included: no delete ("Archive a draft instead."); no change to a signed note or an archived draft; patient, author, supersedes, version and source fixed (42501) |
| [2026-11, `20261113120000`] `clinical_notes_version` (replaced function) | `private.clinical_note_version` | Refuses any `supersedes_id` (42501: a signed note is final); checks `appointment_id` belongs to the patient (22023); sets `version` to 1 |
| [2026-11, `20261113120500`, `20261113120600`] `*_write_audit_log` on `appointment_types`, `clinician_availability`, `call_recordings`; `*_set_updated_at` on `appointment_types` and `call_recordings` | `private.write_audit_log`, `private.set_updated_at` | As above. A booking or cancellation is audited through the existing trigger on `appointments` |
| [2026-11, `a55db7b`] `care_team_reject_self` (before insert or update) | `private.reject_self_care` (definer) | A staff member cannot be put on the care team of their own patient record (42501) |
| [2026-11, `20261114120000`] `care_team_role_matches` (before insert, or update of `staff_id`, `relationship`) | `private.care_team_role_matches` (definer) | The care-team relationship must match the person's `clinical_role` (22023) |
| [2026-11, `20261114120000`] `patient_guardians_guard_verifier` (before update) | `private.guard_guardian_verifier` | For `authenticated`, `verified_by` may change only to the writer's own staff id (42501) |
| [2026-11, `20261114120000`] `staff_profiles_drop_leaver_connections` (after update of `active`, `archived_at`) | `private.drop_leaver_connections` (definer) | On switching a staff member off, deletes their Google Calendar connection as system job `offboarding`. **[2026-11, `20261115120000`]** first copies the encrypted token into `google_revocations` for the server to revoke at Google |
| [2026-11, `20261114120000`] `wearable_readings_check` (before insert) | `private.check_wearable_reading` | No future `measured_on` (London date); value inside the metric's range (22023) |
| [2026-11, `20261114120100`] `question_replies_write_audit_log` | `private.write_audit_log` | As above |
| [2026-11, `20261116120000`] `prescribable_medicines_write_audit_log`, `repeat_request_items_write_audit_log` | `private.write_audit_log` | As above. `repeat_requests_reject_client_changes` does not list the new `repeat_requests` columns; that no longer matters, as `authenticated` has no update grant on the table |
| [2026-11, `20261116120100`, `feat/cqc-audit`] `lab_report_signoffs_write_audit_log` (after insert, update or delete) | `private.write_audit_log` | As above; each sign-off is one `audit_log` row |
| [2026-11, `20261116120100`, `feat/cqc-audit`] `lab_report_signoffs_permanent` (before update or delete, for each row) | `private.reject_signoff_change` (not definer) | Refuses every update and delete, for every role, the owner included (42501, "A sign-off is permanent and cannot be changed or removed."). There is no `before truncate` guard (Finding 134) |
| [2026-11, `20261117120000`] `form_submissions_write_audit_log`, `patient_feedback_write_audit_log`, `feedback_follow_ups_write_audit_log` | `private.write_audit_log` | As above |
| [2026-11, `20261117120000`] `form_submissions_permanent`, `feedback_follow_ups_permanent` (before update or delete, for each row); `form_submissions_no_truncate`, `feedback_follow_ups_no_truncate` (before truncate, for each statement) | `private.reject_permanent_change` (not definer) | Refuses every change for every role, the owner included (42501). Unlike `lab_report_signoffs` (Finding 134), truncate is covered |
| [2026-11, `20261117120000`] `repeat_request_items_record_form` (after insert, for each statement, `referencing new table as new_items`) | `private.record_repeat_request_form` (definer) | Copies each new request to `form_submissions` once its medicines are in |
| [2026-11, `20261117120000`] `questions_record_form` (after insert, for each row) | `private.record_question_form` (definer) | Copies each new question to `form_submissions`. Replies (`question_replies`) are not copied |

Audited tables: every table except `notification_preferences`, `notifications`, `webhook_events`, `integration_sync_log` and `audit_log`.

**Audit rules** [2026-11]: for an update, `metadata` is `{"changed": [column names]}` and **never values**, because the table can never be erased and must not hold clinical data. The actor is `auth.uid()`; server code that acts for a person names them with `set_config('app.actor_id', '<profile id>', true)` in the same transaction. `actor_role` is the person's most specific role (staff over patient). Select-only "viewed" events are not written by triggers. Since `20261102150000` [2026-11, uncommitted], `private.write_audit_log` refuses a write made as the service role that names neither an actor (`app.actor_id`) nor a background job (`app.system_actor`, recorded in `metadata` as `system`). SQL run by the database owner (migrations, seed) is unaffected.

### 4.9 Storage

`20261102111728_storage.sql` and, for signatures, `20261104120100_signatures_bucket.sql` (uncommitted), and for recordings `20261113120600` [2026-11]. [2026-11] Since `20261118120000` there are five buckets, all private; files are served through short-lived signed URLs (3600 seconds for photos and signatures). No delete policy on any bucket: files are not hard-deleted, so a removed photo or a replaced file stays in storage and is simply no longer shown.

| Bucket | Limits | Path | Policies |
|---|---|---|---|
| `documents` | 25 MiB; pdf, jpeg, png, heic | `<patient id>/...`, or `unmatched/...` before matching | Select: anyone who can read the matching `documents` row under their own RLS. Insert: someone who can act for the patient, or a doctor or PA (for `unmatched/` or a patient in scope) |
| `avatars` | 2 MiB; jpeg, png, webp | exactly `<user id>/avatar` for new writes; older objects may have other names | Select: own, any active staff member's, or team reads all (patient photos therefore reach every doctor, PA and admin, not the coach or a guardian). Insert and update: own folder, exactly the name `avatar` (`docs/SECURITY.md` S-70) |
| `signatures` [2026-11, uncommitted] | 1 MiB; png, jpeg, webp | exactly `<user id>/signature` | Select: the owner only. Insert and update: the owner, and only a doctor, at that exact name. Letters will read it with the service role in server code |
| `education` [2026-11, `20261118120000`] | 20 MiB; pdf, png, jpeg, webp | `courses/<course id>/cover-<uuid>.<png\|jpg\|webp>` or `files/<lesson id>/<uuid>.<pdf\|png\|jpg>` (the actions make the path) | Select: `can_read_education_object(name)`: clinical staff and admins every object; a patient or guardian the cover of a course given to them and the unarchived files of lessons they can read. Insert: admins only, at one of the two path shapes (the policy does not check that the course or lesson exists, Finding 164). No update or delete: a new cover or file is a new object and the old one stays. Served by one-hour signed URLs made through the reader's session (`lib/data/education.ts`) |
| `recordings` [2026-11, `20261113120600`] | 50 MiB; webm, ogg, mp4, mpeg, wav audio | `<patient id>/<uuid>.<webm\|ogg\|m4a>` (the action makes the path) | Select: staff with the patient in scope (folder name through `try_uuid`). Insert: a doctor, nurse or health coach with the patient in scope; it does not require a matching `call_recordings` row (Finding 97). No update or delete. The browser uploads through a signed upload URL made with the person's own session; the server gives AssemblyAI a one-hour signed read URL made with the service role |

[2026-11] Changed policies on `feat/clinic-build-2`:
- `documents` insert (`20261113120000`): someone who can act for the patient; a PA or **admin** for `unmatched/` (doctors no longer); a doctor, **nurse** or PA for a patient in scope. The nurse half resolves Finding 71.
- `avatars` select (`20261113120100`): one's own folder; or, for active staff only, any active staff member's photo and the photos of patients in `in_scope` (PAs and admins every patient, doctors, nurses and coaches their care team's). **Patients and guardians see only their own photo**, never staff photos (decided 11 November, knowledge base question 23). The earlier row above ("own, any active staff member's, or team reads all") is superseded.
- `recordings` insert (`20261114120000`, S-126): a file may be written only at the exact `storage_path` of a `call_recordings` row the uploader opened (`recorded_by` = own staff id), while it is `uploading`, for a patient in scope. The role is no longer named in the policy; only doctors, nurses and coaches can open such a row. Finding 97's first point is resolved.
- `documents` [2026-11, `feat/finalise-points`]: no policy change. Letters, referrals and other documents are uploaded by doctors, nurses and PAs through their own session at `<patient id>/<uuid>.<pdf|jpg|png>` under the existing insert policy, and served by 60-second signed URLs made through the viewer's session (section 6).

### 4.10 Extensions, schemas and API exposure

- `private` schema: usage granted to `authenticated` and, since the throttle migration, `service_role` [2026-11]; not exposed through the Data API; `anon` has nothing. `service_role` holds execute on `private.auth_throttle_hit` and, since `20261111120000` [2026-11], `private.save_google_calendar_connection` and `private.remove_google_calendar_connection`, and nothing on the throttle table. [2026-11] Since `feat/clinic-build-2` also `private.set_patient_ghl_contact`, `private.set_appointment_calendar_event`, `private.recording_sent`, `private.recording_failed` and `private.recording_transcribed`.
- `pg_graphql` is **dropped** and `graphql_public` is removed from the exposed schemas [2026-11]. `[api] schemas = ["public"]` in `supabase/config.toml`.
- pgTAP is created inside the tests.

### 4.12 Tests, seed and local commands

- `supabase/tests/00_schema_checks.test.sql` (pgTAP): table count, forced RLS, no `FOR ALL`, no cascades, no anon grants, delete grants, `search_path` on every function, no definer in `public`, and more.
- `supabase/tests/01_role_matrix.test.sql` (pgTAP): runs SQL as each seeded user the way PostgREST does and checks what each role can read and write.
- `supabase/tests/02_auth_throttle.test.sql` (pgTAP) [2026-11]: anon and authenticated cannot call the throttle or read the counters, the service role reaches the counters only through the function, a full bucket refuses, a refused attempt counts nowhere, an expired window restarts. Uncommitted.
- `supabase/tests/03_scope_and_audit_actor.test.sql` (pgTAP) [2026-11]: the scoped policies still admit clinical staff, and the audit trigger refuses a service-role write with no actor or system job, accepts one that names either, and leaves database-owner SQL alone. Uncommitted. `01_role_matrix` now sets `app.system_actor` for its service-role writes.

### 4.13 Auth configuration (`supabase/config.toml`)

- `[auth] enable_signup = false`: public sign-up is off [2026-11].
- `[auth.email] enable_signup = true` **must stay true**: if it is false, email sign-in is disabled altogether [2026-11].
- Password 12 to 128 characters (shared Zod rules in `lib/auth/password-rules.ts`; Auth config minimum 12), with lower case, upper case and digits; refresh-token rotation on; email confirmations off locally; anonymous sign-ins off; MFA off (deferred, see section 8); the recovery, email-change and reauthentication emails and the two notifications (password changed, and `[auth.email.notification.email_changed]`, which tells the old address when the sign-in email changes) use the branded files in `supabase/templates/` (built by `pnpm email:build`, section 5), with both notifications enabled [2026-11].
- Site URL `http://localhost:3000`; redirects allowed on port 3000 only (see Findings on `web-local`).
- Resend SMTP (`smtp.resend.com`, port 465, user `resend`, the API key as password) is present as **commented** settings for hosted use; locally, mail goes to Mailpit. The hosted project is set by hand (section 2) [2026-11].
- `[auth.sessions]` stays commented out: Supabase's time-box and inactivity settings need the Pro plan, so the proxy enforces the limits instead (section 5).
- These settings describe the local stack. The hosted project is configured by hand: public sign-up off, minimum password 12, and leaked password protection off because it needs the Pro plan [2026-11].

### 4.14 Changes from `docs/reference/schema-draft.md` [2026-11]

1. `documents.patient_id` is nullable (emailed reports before matching; path `unmatched/`), and such rows are staff-only (doctor and PA) until matched. [2026-11] Since `20261113120000`: PA and admin only (item 23).
2. Invitations are created only by server code, through the functions in 4.7a. The draft says "PA, admin create", but the token must not be chosen by the inviter. PAs read patient and guardian invitations and admins read all, without `token_hash`; a guardian invitation has no role (`invitations.role` is nullable) [2026-11].
3. `created_at` added to every table; `archived_at` added as briefed (staff_profiles, patient_profiles, diagnoses, medications, documents, lab_panels).
4. Extra enums for fixed lists the draft left as text (17 of the 32).
5. Composite foreign keys: results to lab_reports, repeat_requests to medications, lab_reports to documents, so related rows always belong to the same patient.
6. `app_role` has no guardian.
7. Clinical staff, team and admin are defined as in 4.2; admin is deliberately not clinical; the health coach reads released data only and no team-only tables. [2026-11] Superseded in part by item 18: admins and in-scope coaches now read the whole record.
8. `pg_graphql` dropped; `graphql_public` removed from exposed schemas.
9. Read functions split into a private definer body and a public invoker wrapper; `get_staff_directory()` replaces the `staff_directory` view and returns `profile_id`.
10. One permissive policy per command per table.
11. Audit metadata stores changed column names only; `app.actor_id` for server code.
12. Service role has no delete or truncate on clinical tables.
13. Extra defences not in the draft: `reject_client_changes` triggers, the paid-status trigger, column-level grants, forced RLS, the 14-day invitation expiry, and the guardian-link rules (a PA cannot link or verify in someone else's name).
14. `private.auth_throttle` and the `auth_throttle_hit` functions are not in the draft; they exist to limit sign-in and reset attempts (see 4.15).
15. [2026-11] `marker_conversions`, `results.original_value`, `results.original_unit`, the `staff_upload` value of `document_source` and the functions in 4.7b are not in the draft (`20261109120000`).
16. [2026-11, `20261110120000` and `20261110120100`] Not in the draft: the `nurse` value of `app_role` and `staff_role` (a nurse was not clinical staff until item 18); `patient_profiles.gender_identity` (at most 60 characters) with `sex` documented as sex at birth; the `viewed` audit action written by `log_record_view`, and the admin-only `activity_log` function, so an admin now reads `audit_log` through that function (the draft has the log server-only); `invitations_kind_fields` allows `nurse` for team invitations.

17. [2026-11, `20261111120000`] Not in the draft: the `google_calendar_connections` table (keyed by `user_id`, no `id` and no `created_at`, against item 3), its service-role-only save and remove functions, and a hard delete on disconnect (the draft archives clinical records; this row is a credential). The draft's only calendar field is `locations.ghl_calendar_id`.
18. [2026-11, `20261112120000` and `20261112120100`] `care_relationship` gains `nurse`. `care_team` limits who opens a record, as the draft intends, but by role: PAs and admins are not limited by it, and doctors, nurses and health coaches are; the draft reads it by the team only and writes it by PA and doctor, while the build also lets in-scope nurses and coaches read it and admins write it; admins read every patient's clinical record and PAs every patient (the draft and item 7 above kept admins out); the nurse is clinical staff; the health coach is no longer held to released data within their care team.
19. [2026-11, `20261112120200`] Doctors send patient invitations for their own patients, and a new patient a doctor invites is put on the doctor's care team (the draft has "PA, admin create").
20. [2026-11, `20261112120300`] Not in the draft: the `clinical_notes` table, the `note_status` and `note_source` enums, `sign_clinical_note`, and the version and lock triggers. The draft has notes only as free-text `notes` columns (`diagnoses.notes`); consultation notes were left to Semble.
21. [2026-11, `20261112120400`] Not in the draft: `education_courses`, `education_sections`, `education_modules`, `education_assignments`, `wearable_readings`, the `wearable_metric` enum and `course_is_mine`.
22. [2026-11, `20261113120000`] A signed clinical note is final: the correction mechanism of item 20 (a new version superseding a signed note) is switched off, though `supersedes_id` and `version` stay as columns.
23. [2026-11, `20261113120000`] Item 1 changes: unmatched `documents` rows and imports are for **PAs and admins** only, not doctors. The draft has no rule for unmatched files.
24. [2026-11, `20261113120100`] Photos: the draft lets the team read every profile (avatar included) and says nothing on patients seeing staff photos. The build lets staff see team photos and in-scope patients' photos, and patients only their own; `get_staff_directory` hides `avatar_path` from non-staff.
25. [2026-11, `20261113120200`] `patient_guardians` write: the draft has "PA, after verifying"; the build also lets admins write, and lets any PA or admin remove (switch off) a link verified by someone else.
26. [2026-11, `20261113120300`] `ghl_contact_id` is in the draft; the service-role-only `set_patient_ghl_contact` function that writes it is not.
27. [2026-11, `20261113120400`, `20261113120500`] Not in the draft: the `clinic_hub` value of `appt_source`, `appointment_types`, `clinician_availability`, `appointments.appointment_type_id` and `booked_by`, and the five booking functions. The draft has appointments written by "Server sync only"; the build lets patients, acting guardians and in-scope staff book through `book_appointment` and staff cancel through `cancel_appointment` (Finding 90). For `clinic_hub` rows `external_id` holds a Google Calendar event id.
28. [2026-11, `20261113120600`] Not in the draft: `call_recordings`, the `recording_status` enum, the `recordings` bucket and the three recording functions. The draft names Fathom in `webhook_source` and `sync_system`; Fathom is replaced by AssemblyAI, which has no value there.
29. [2026-11, `20261114120100`] Not in the draft: the `question_replies` table and the `reply_to_question` and `close_question` functions; status changes only through them (no update policy or grant on `questions` since `09969cc`); `log_record_view` takes `documents`. **Conflict:** the draft says each question "opens a conversation in GoHighLevel, where the team replies"; the build keeps the whole conversation in Clinic Hub and never writes `questions.ghl_conversation_id` (Finding 112). The draft has question status changed by the team in scope; the functions let any staff member in scope reply and close, health coaches included.
30. [2026-11, `20261114120000`] Not in the draft: the triggers that tie a care-team relationship to the person's role, guard a guardian link's verifier, drop a leaver's Google connection, and check wearable readings in the database; `register_import` and the `recordings` upload policy follow the rules above. `patient_guardians` keeps one row per guardian and patient, reactivated on a new invitation.
31. [2026-11, earlier edits on `origin/dev`] Not in the draft: `in_scope` excludes the caller's own record and those they look after; `private.london_today()`; `private.is_my_clinician`, which narrows `clinician_availability` reads for patients; the unique index on `patient_profiles.ghl_contact_id`; the three-booking cap for patients.
32. [2026-11, `20261115120000`] Not in the draft: `patient_allergies.archived_at` (the draft has no archive column on allergies; archiving follows the no-delete rule) and the `google_revocations` table (a server-only queue with no `updated_at`, no `archived_at` and no audit trigger; it holds a credential, not a clinical record, and the token is cleared once revoked).
33. [2026-11, `20261116120000`] Not in the draft: `prescribable_medicines`; `repeat_request_items` (a request holds up to ten medicines, so `repeat_requests.medication_id` becomes nullable, against the draft's one medicine per request); on `repeat_requests` the Typeform answers, `paid_at`, `stripe_checkout_session_id`, `cancelled_at`, `decided_by` and `decision_note`; `medications.run_out_reminded_for`; and the functions in 4.7j. The draft's writes (insert by the patient side, status by PA and doctor, payment by the Stripe webhook) become function-only writes, and the patient side, a PA or a doctor withdrawing an unpaid request. (The first commit also let admins move the status and withdraw; the review fixes removed that, matching the draft's PA and doctor.) The draft has the request read by a GoHighLevel task; no task is created and `ghl_task_id` stays unused (Finding 124).
34. [2026-11, `20261116120100`, `feat/cqc-audit`] Not in the draft: the `lab_report_signoffs` table (keyed by `report_id`, with `signed_at` instead of `created_at`, against item 3), its permanence trigger, the sign-off arguments of `release_report`, and the five treatment-option columns on `clinical_notes`. The draft's `lab_reports` has `reviewed_by` and `reviewed_at` only, with no outcome or action. Asked for by the clinic's CQC governance adviser (Dr Schoeman, 12 November 2026). `outcome` is a checked `text`, not an enum (Finding 137).
35. [2026-11, `20261117120000`, `feat/patient-feedback`] Not in the draft: the `form_kind` enum and the `form_submissions`, `patient_feedback` and `feedback_follow_ups` tables, with their functions and triggers. `form_submissions.source_id` points at one of three tables and so has no foreign key; `feedback_follow_ups` is keyed by `feedback_id`, has `followed_up_at` instead of `created_at` (against item 3) and an `id` column kept equal to the key for the audit log; `patient_feedback.rating` is a generated `text`, not an enum (against item 4's practice). **Conflict:** the earlier knowledge-base decision (12 November 2026) placed feedback in GoHighLevel, which `CLAUDE.md` says keeps marketing; the clinic then decided the same day to build it in Clinic Hub (`content/knowledge-base.md`, decisions). Feedback is read by admins, PAs and doctors but not nurses or health coaches, a narrower rule than the `in_scope` reads of most clinical tables.
36. [2026-11, `20261117120100`, `feat/patient-feedback`] Not in the draft: `patient_feedback.quote_checked_by` and `quote_checked_at`, a both-or-neither pair rather than a status enum; the bank-holiday functions `easter_sunday` and `is_bank_holiday`; `check_feedback_quote` and `log_feedback_export`. The export's audit row has action `exported` and no `entity_id`, a whole-table entry rather than the per-row entries the audit triggers write.
37. [2026-11, `20261118120000`, `feat/education-builder`] Not in the draft (which has no education at all, item 21): `education_courses.cover_path`, `education_modules.content` and `published`, the `education_files` and `education_progress` tables, `lesson_is_mine`, `reorder_course`, `can_read_education_object` and the `education` bucket. `education_files` has no `updated_at` and `education_progress` has `completed_at` instead of `created_at` and no archive column, against item 3 and the opening rule of 4.4 (Finding 163). A lesson's text is a JSON document whose shape is enforced by server code, not the database.
38. [2026-11, `20261119120000`, `feat/clinic-diary`] Not in the draft: `appointment_types.bookable_online`, `patient_profiles.booked_online_at`, `appointments.booked_online`, and the functions `clinic_diary`, `move_appointment`, `online_booking_clinicians`, `online_busy` and `book_online`. The draft has no public (signed-out) way in and has `anon` holding nothing; the build keeps `anon` at nothing in the database but adds a signed-out booking page whose server creates `patient_profiles` and `appointments` rows through the service role (Findings 166 and 167). The draft's patient record is created by an invitation; an online booking now creates one with no invitation, no account and no care team. The draft scopes appointment reads to the patient record level; `clinic_diary` shows every appointment, with the patient's name, to every staff role (Finding 168). **Update [2026-11, `20261121120000`]:** `clinic_diary` now shows every appointment to admins and PAs only (who already read every patient) and a clinician's own bookings to that clinician, care team or not; `book_online` no longer always creates a record (4.7p).
39. [2026-11, `20261120120000`, `signaturerx`] Not in the draft: the patient's postal address columns on `patient_profiles`; `signaturerx_prescribers` (keyed by `user_id`, with no `id`, against item 3) and `signaturerx_prescriptions`; and the functions in 4.7o (the second migration, `20261120120100`, only replaces `request_repeat`; the third, `20261120120200`, adds the two functions that settle a stuck send; the fourth, `20261120120300`, is a lint fix to `reorder_course`; the first three were first numbered 35 here, before the merge, and were applied on the hosted project under the old names `20261117120000`, `20261117120100` and `20261118120000`). The draft has `medications.semble_prescription_id` and no SignatureRx reference; the clinic decided prescribing goes through SignatureRx (`CLAUDE.md`). Clinic Hub holds only the reference and state, not the prescription.
40. [2026-11, `20261122120000`, `feat/lab-webhook`] Not in the draft: a push webhook for lab files. The draft names a `tdl` value in `webhook_source` and `document_source` and plans a lab feed per lab; the migration adds a generic `lab_feed` value instead, with the lab named in `result_imports.source_provider`, and the seven `source_*` columns on `result_imports`, the unique index, and the functions in 4.7q. The draft's `result_imports` has no such columns. `tdl` stays, unused.

### 4.15 Attempt limits (`20261102143000_auth_throttle.sql`) [2026-11]

- `private.auth_throttle`: `key_hash` (text, pk), `attempts` (integer, check > 0), `window_ends_at` (indexed), `created_at`. Keys are SHA-256 hashes made by the server, so the table holds no email or IP address. All privileges revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated` and `service_role`. It is in `private`, outside the Data API, and has no RLS: the revokes are its protection.
- `private.auth_throttle_hit(_keys text[], _limits integer[], _window_seconds integer) returns boolean`: `SECURITY DEFINER`, `search_path = ''`. Purges expired rows; returns false if any bucket is full, counting nothing, so a refused attempt cannot use up another bucket; otherwise adds one to every bucket and returns true. Concurrent calls can overshoot a limit slightly.
- `public.auth_throttle_hit(...)`: `SECURITY INVOKER` wrapper, execute granted to `service_role` only. Called from `lib/auth/throttle.ts`.

| `app/globals.css` | Built. Every design token and type utility |
| `app/dev/ui/` | Built. Component gallery |

The root not-found page: wordmark, eyebrow, title "We could not find that page", one button to `/` (which sends each person to their own area, or to sign-in). Answers HTTP 404 and is `noindex, nofollow`. It also renders when a page calls `notFound()` for a record the person may not open (for example a doctor opening another doctor's patient), with the same copy, so it never says whether the record exists. It sits outside the clinic and portal shells (no sidebar or top bar). No migration, no new route entry in the build output, no environment variable |

Shared by both knowledge base pages. `requireKnowledgeBaseReader(returnPath)`: no user redirects to `/login?next=<returnPath>`; not admin or suspended goes to `homePathFor` (else `/login?error=no-access`). `KnowledgeBaseShell` (`activePath`, `source`, `titleBefore`, `titleAccent`, `intro`): staff `SessionGuard`, back link, wordmark, title, two tabs ("How it works" `/knowledge-base`, "Try the results flow" `/knowledge-base/test-results`), the `##` contents list, `Markdown`, footer |

[2026-11, `feat/small-items`] `google.ts` adds `tryRevokeToken(token)`: POST to Google's revoke endpoint with the usual timeout, true when Google answers OK **or 400** (a token Google no longer knows counts as revoked), false on a network failure or any other error (the older `revokeToken` stays best effort). `leavers.ts` (`server-only`): `revokeLeaverTokens()` reads up to 50 unrevoked rows of `google_revocations` with fewer than 10 attempts (`MAX_ATTEMPTS`), oldest first, through the service role; decrypts each token with the row's `user_id` and calls `tryRevokeToken`; on success sets `revoked_at` and clears the token, otherwise adds one to `attempts` (an undecryptable token counts as a failed try; a row with no token counts as done). Returns `{ revoked, waiting }` for that batch |

For a `clinic_hub` appointment: on a move, deletes the old event from the previous clinician's calendar (`previous.clinicianUserId`, `previous.eventId`); then creates an event in the current clinician's calendar for a booking or a move (title "<type> · <clinic reference>") and stores its id, or deletes it on a cancellation. GoHighLevel API v2 (`https://services.leadconnectorhq.com`, header `Version: 2021-07-28`, Private Integration bearer token, 10-second timeout): `upsertContact` (`/contacts/upsert` by email or phone in `GHL_LOCATION_ID`), `sendEmail` (`/conversations/messages`, type `Email`), `sendWhatsApp` (type `SMS`; the clinic's plug-in diverts texts to WhatsApp). `GhlError` names the call only, never echoes GoHighLevel's reply |
| `lib/notify/patient.ts` | [2026-11]. `server-only`. `notifyPatient(patientId, { topic, email, whatsapp })`, the one place patient messages go out. Uses the service role to read the patient (`contact_email`, `contact_phone`, `ghl_contact_id`), the account (`email`, `phone`, `status`) and `notification_preferences`. A suspended account gets nothing. Address: the account email, else `contact_email`; phone: `contact_phone`, else the account phone. Email is on unless the person saved it off; WhatsApp is off unless they turned it on. With GoHighLevel configured both go through it (contact upserted and its id stored through `set_patient_ghl_contact` on first use); without it email goes through Resend and WhatsApp is not sent. Each GoHighLevel attempt writes an `integration_sync_log` row (`system` ghl, `direction` out, `entity` patient_profiles, `ok` or `failed`); Resend sends are not logged. Never throws; returns a `Delivery` per channel. Writes no in-portal `notifications` row. **Changed [2026-11]:** (`29f602f`) the GoHighLevel contact is upserted afresh on **every** send with today's details, sending only the email when email is wanted and only the phone when WhatsApp is wanted; if the contact id differs from the stored one it is stored through `set_patient_ghl_contact`, and if that fails (the unique index: the contact belongs to another patient) GoHighLevel is not used for this patient: email goes through Resend and WhatsApp is reported `not-connected`. (`2847f26`) A suspended account returns `suspended` on both channels (was `off`). A new optional `notice` (`title`, `body`, `link`) writes an in-portal `notifications` row with the service role, for patients with an account, unless they saved the `portal` channel off for the topic; it is written before any email or WhatsApp. Callers with a notice: booking and cancelling, a clinic reply to a question, a shared document. Result release passes none (`release_report` writes its own). **[2026-11, uncommitted, `fix/online-booking-confirmation`]** New optional `alwaysEmail`: when true the email is wanted even if the person saved appointment emails off (`wantsEmail = alwaysEmail || (choice("email") ?? true)`); it does not override a suspended account (still `suspended` on both channels), a failed read of the choices, a missing address, or the route (GoHighLevel when configured and `resendOnly` is not set, else Resend). Only online-booking confirmations use it. A Resend refusal is now also logged with `console.error("Patient email not sent: Resend refused it.")`, with no address or patient detail, because Resend sends are not in `integration_sync_log` |

**Session limits** (`lib/supabase/session-cookies.ts`, enforced in `lib/supabase/proxy.ts`) [2026-11, uncommitted]:

| Kind | Total | Idle |
|---|---|---|
| Staff | 12 hours | 30 minutes |
| Patient (and guardian) | 7 days | 2 hours |

- `ch_session_kind` holds the kind and `ch_last_seen` the last request time; both are **signed** [2026-11, uncommitted]. The value is `value.signature`, where the signature is HMAC-SHA256 (Web Crypto, key `SESSION_COOKIE_SECRET`) over `cookie name|user id|value`, so a cookie cannot be edited, moved to another cookie or used by another account. A missing, unsigned, edited or foreign cookie reads as missing: a missing last-seen counts as expired and a missing kind gets the staff limits. If `SESSION_COOKIE_SECRET` is missing or under 32 characters, `getSessionEnv` throws and the proxy fails closed. Server code sets them through `startSessionCookies` in `lib/auth/session-start.ts`.
- `signIn` and `updatePassword` set it; staff limits win for someone who is also a patient, and a missing or unknown value gets the staff limits. Paths under `/clinic` always use the staff limits, whatever the cookie says.
- `ch_last_seen` is written at most once a minute. Sign-in and password change set it. A missing value, or one more than a minute in the future, counts as expired, so the visitor is signed out.
- The total runs from the latest sign-in time in the token's `amr` claim, which a refresh does not change, with `iat` as the fallback.
- A session past a limit is signed out locally, its `sb-*` and `ch_*` cookies are removed, and the visitor goes to `/login?error=expired`.
- `/auth/confirm` is exempt in the proxy, which leaves both `ch_` cookies alone there. The route itself calls `startSessionCookies(user id, null)`, which signs `ch_last_seen` as now and deletes `ch_session_kind`, only after `verifyOtp` or `exchangeCodeForSession` succeeds, because a verified link starts a new session (staff limits apply until the kind is known). A bad link changes nothing.
- Auth cookies and both `ch_` cookies are `HttpOnly`, `Secure` in production, `SameSite=Lax`, with a 7-day `maxAge`. `@supabase/ssr` overwrites `maxAge` with its 400-day default, so `withSessionMaxAge` sets it again on each write.

**Session guard** (`components/shell/session-guard.tsx`) [2026-11, uncommitted]. A Client Component rendered by `AppShell`, which has a new required prop `sessionKind` (`staff` or `patient`): the clinic layout passes `staff`, the portal layout passes `staff` for anyone with a staff role and `patient` otherwise. While the person is active (pointer, key, scroll, touch) it calls `router.refresh()` at most every 5 minutes, which keeps the idle clock moving; once the person stops for the session's idle limit, an idle timer calls `router.refresh()` so the proxy ends the session and sends them to `/login?error=expired`. Without it an abandoned screen stays readable. It renders nothing and makes no Supabase call.

**Content-Security-Policy** (`lib/csp.ts`, set in `lib/supabase/proxy.ts`) [2026-11, uncommitted]. The proxy makes a nonce for each request (`createNonce`), builds the policy with `contentSecurityPolicy(nonce)`, and sets it on every response (including redirects) and, with `x-nonce`, on the forwarded request headers so Next.js adds the nonce to its scripts. `script-src` is `'self' 'nonce-...' 'strict-dynamic'`, with `'unsafe-eval'` in development only. `style-src` still allows `'unsafe-inline'`, because React and Radix write style attributes a nonce cannot cover (`docs/SECURITY.md` S-12). Other directives: `default-src 'self'`, images from self, `data:`, `blob:` and the Supabase origin, `connect-src` self plus Supabase (and local websockets in development), `object-src 'none'`, `base-uri 'self'`, `form-action 'self'`, `frame-ancestors 'none'`. [2026-11, `fc7b5e8`] `frame-src` is now set explicitly to `VIDEO_FRAME_SOURCES` (`https://www.youtube-nocookie.com`, `https://player.vimeo.com`, `https://www.loom.com`) on every page, so only those players can be framed (not even `'self'`); no other screen uses a frame. A nonce needs a fresh render, so `app/layout.tsx` awaits `connection()` and no page is statically rendered. [2026-11, `49cb8b8`] `frame-src` now lists `https://challenges.cloudflare.com` after the three video players, for the Turnstile widget on `/book/details`; its script loads through the nonce and `'strict-dynamic'`, so `script-src` is unchanged. The server's `siteverify` call is not subject to the CSP.

## 6. Routes, server actions and API routes

| Route | State |
|---|---|
| `/` | Redirects: signed-out to `/login`, staff to `/clinic`, patient or guardian to `/portal` |
| `/login`, `/forgot-password`, `/update-password` | Built. `/login?error=` takes `link`, `no-access` and, new and uncommitted, `expired` [2026-11]; any other value shows nothing |
| `/auth/confirm` | Route handler (GET), built |
| `/portal` | Built. Patient home |
| `/clinic` | Built. Clinic dashboard; optional `?location=<id>` |
| `/portal/profile`, `/clinic/profile` | Built, uncommitted [2026-11]. The same screen under each area, with `?tab=`: `details`, `record` (patients), `guardianship` (guardians), `work` and `signature` (staff; the signature only for doctors), `notifications`, `security`. An unknown tab shows details. The tabs a person sees depend on the area and their roles: a patient has Details, Your record, Notifications and Security; a guardian adds People you look after (and has no record if not a patient); a doctor, PA, health coach or admin has Details, Work profile, Notifications and Security, a doctor also Signature; a doctor who is also a patient has both areas, each with its own tabs. Reached from the account menu and from the crumb |
| `/dev/ui` | Component gallery; `notFound()` when `NODE_ENV` is production |
| `/knowledge-base/test-results` | Built, uncommitted [2026-11]. Admins only, `noindex`; second tab, renders `content/test-results-flow.md`. Signed out: proxy 307 to `/login?next=%2Fknowledge-base%2Ftest-results`. Everyone else, and suspended people, go to their own home |
| `/knowledge-base` | Built, uncommitted [2026-11]. Admins only, `noindex`; renders `content/knowledge-base.md`. Signed out: proxy 307 to `/login?next=%2Fknowledge-base`. Everyone else, and suspended people, go to their own home |
| `/invite` | Route handler (GET), built, uncommitted [2026-11]. Public. Takes `?token=`; if it is exactly 43 base64url characters it sets the cookie `ch_invite` (`HttpOnly`, `Secure` in production, `SameSite=Lax`, `Path=/invite`, one hour) and in every case redirects to `/invite/accept` with no query, `Cache-Control: no-store`. It reads nothing from the database and uses nothing up, so a mail scanner that follows the link harms nothing. The route also sets `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, but the global header in `next.config.ts` wins (`docs/SECURITY.md` S-82) |
| `/invite/accept` | Built, uncommitted [2026-11]. Public page, rendered per request, reads `ch_invite`. | `/api/google/connect` | Route handler (GET), built, uncommitted [2026-11]. Signed-out, suspended or non-staff: redirect to `/login`. Google variables missing: redirect to `/clinic/calendar?error=not-set-up`. Otherwise makes a 32-byte base64url nonce, sets `ch_google_state` = `<user id>.<nonce>` (`HttpOnly`, `Secure` in production, `SameSite=Lax`, `Path=/api/google`, 10 minutes; not HMAC-signed) and redirects to Google's consent screen with the nonce as `state` and the redirect URI `<SITE_URL>/api/google/callback` |
| `/api/google/callback` | Route handler (GET), built, uncommitted [2026-11]. Same user checks. The cookie's user id must be the signed-in person and `state` must equal the cookie's nonce (constant-time compare), else `?error=expired`; then Google's `error` gives `?error=cancelled` and a missing code `?error=failed`. Exchanges the code, refuses (and revokes) a grant without the calendar scope (`?error=no-calendar`), encrypts the refresh token and calls `save_google_calendar_connection` with the service role; if the save fails the token is revoked at Google. Every outcome clears the cookie and redirects to `/clinic/calendar?connected=1` or `?error=…`. Logs name the step, never a token or code |
