| `/api/cron/revoke-google` | Route handler (GET), built [2026-11, `feat/small-items`]. Run daily by Vercel Cron (`vercel.json`), which sends `Authorization: Bearer <CRON_SECRET>`. Not under the proxy's protected prefixes; no session. 404 if `CRON_SECRET` is missing or shorter than 32 characters; 401 unless the header matches (constant-time compare). Calls `revokeLeaverTokens()` and answers JSON `{ revoked, waiting }`; 500 if it throws, logged without detail |
| `/api/stripe/webhook` | Route handler (POST), built [2026-11, `feat/repeat-prescriptions`]. Not under the proxy's protected prefixes; no session. 404 unless Stripe is configured. 400 for a body over 256,000 characters, a bad signature, bad JSON or an unexpected shape (Zod). Acts only on `checkout.session.completed` and `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded` (others 200), and only for a session that is `paid`, in GBP, with an amount and a request id (metadata first, then `client_reference_id`); otherwise 200. Stores the event in `webhook_events` (source `stripe`; payload session id, request id and amount only); a duplicate event id is finished only if still `received`. Calls `mark_repeat_request_paid` with the service role: on error the event is marked `failed` and the answer is still 200; otherwise `processed`, and a new payment calls `announceRequest(requestId, { paid: true })` |
| `/api/cron/prescription-reminders` | **Removed [2026-11, `feat/patient-feedback`]**: replaced by `/api/cron/patient-messages`. Earlier: route handler (GET), built [2026-11, `feat/repeat-prescriptions`]. Run daily at `0 9 * * *` (09:00 UTC) by Vercel Cron. The same `CRON_SECRET` gate as `/api/cron/revoke-google` (404 without a secret of 32 characters or more, 401 on a mismatch, constant-time). Calls `sendRunOutReminders()` and answers JSON `{ patients, medicines }`; 500 if it throws, logged without detail |
| `/api/cron/patient-messages` | Route handler (GET), built [2026-11, `feat/patient-feedback`]; **replaces** `/api/cron/prescription-reminders` (above, deleted). Run daily at `0 9 * * *` (09:00 UTC) by Vercel Cron. The same `CRON_SECRET` gate (404 without a secret of 32 characters or more, 401 on a mismatch, constant-time). Runs `sendRunOutReminders()` and `sendFeedbackSurveys()` together (`Promise.allSettled`), so one failing does not stop the other; answers JSON `{ reminders, feedback }`, each null if its run failed, with 500 if either failed (logged without detail)
| `/api/transcription/webhook` | Route handler (POST), built [2026-11]. Not under the proxy's protected prefixes; no session. 404 if transcription is not configured; 401 unless header `x-clinic-hub-secret` equals `TRANSCRIPTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (constant-time compare); 400 without a `transcript_id` of at most 100 characters. Looks the recording up by `provider_job_id` with the service role (200 and nothing done if unknown), then `completeRecording`, which **fetches** the transcript from AssemblyAI rather than trusting the body. Always 200 after that; a failure is logged without detail. **Changed [2026-11, `2847f26`]:** 503 when `completeRecording` returns `working`, and 500 when it throws, so AssemblyAI retries; 200 for `done`, `failed` and an unknown job |
| `/api/webhooks/lab-results/[provider]` | Route handler (POST), built [2026-11, `feat/lab-webhook`, uncommitted]. Not under the proxy's protected prefixes; no session. `provider` is lower-cased and must be a name in `LAB_WEBHOOK_SECRETS`; an unknown provider, or the webhook switched off, gets an empty **404**. The secret is read from `Authorization: Bearer <secret>` and compared as SHA-256 digests with `timingSafeEqual`; wrong or missing is **401**. Then the rate limit `lab-webhook` per provider (600 an hour, the same for address, address and account, and account), **429** over it (it runs after the secret check, so a wrong secret does not use up the allowance). Body: `multipart/form-data` (text fields plus a file, taking the field named like `file`, `pdf` or `report`, otherwise the first file) or `application/json` (one level of nesting is read as flat; the PDF is base64 in a field such as `file`, `pdf` or `base64`, with or without a `data:` prefix, URL-safe base64 accepted); any other content type is **415**, an unreadable body **400**, no PDF **400**, over 10 MB **413**, not starting with the PDF magic bytes **422**, neither a patient email nor a name **422**. The report's name, email, date of birth, report id, sample date and test name are read by `lib/labs/parse.ts`. `receiveLabFile` then files it; a new file triggers `announceLabFile`. The answer is **200** with `{ status: "accepted" }` or `{ status: "duplicate" }` and never says whether a patient was found; any storage or database failure is **500** (nothing from the request is logged) so the lab retries. A report sent twice is recorded once, keyed by the lab's report id or, when it sends none, a hash of the file. Nothing is released and no result is created: a PA enters the values from the PDF and a doctor releases, as for any imported file |

For doctors and PAs only (uncommitted [2026-11]; before, anyone through the gate): the decision form while open, with a warning when unpaid, and Withdraw while unpaid. Admins read only, except that [2026-11, third pass] the page passes `canResolve` (doctor or admin) so they can settle a send stuck at `sending` | [2026-11, `signaturerx`] A `SignatureRxSection` is added for doctors and PAs (from `getSignatureRxPanel`): a missing-details list, the doctor's integration code field (asked once), the medicines, a choice of draft or sign and issue, the PIN, and the prescriptions already sent with a refresh button. The form shows only when the request is approved and `SIGNATURERX_SIGNING_ENABLED` is on; the whole section depends on the three SignatureRx variables.
| `/clinic/prescriptions/medicines` | Built [2026-11]. Admin or doctor (others to `/clinic/prescriptions`). The clinic's list with archived entries: add (name and a weight-loss box), take off and put back |
| `/portal/feedback/[id]` | Built [2026-11, `feat/patient-feedback`]. 404 for a non-UUID, a survey the session cannot read, or one whose patient is not among the person's own portal patients (`getPortalPatients`), so a staff member cannot open it as staff. Answered: "Thank you" and, if `GOOGLE_REVIEW_URL` is set, a "Leave a Google review" link (new tab, `noopener noreferrer`). [2026-11, `38a918f`] The link is offered **only for a green answer** (9 or 10). [2026-11, `1663764`] The thank-you text adds "Someone from the clinic will be in touch soon." for a red answer or when the patient asked to be contacted, and "We read every answer and use it to improve." for amber; the page links home. Expired: "This survey has closed". Otherwise the survey form if the person can act, else a notice that only the patient or a guardian who can act may answer. Reached from the message link, the portal notice or the home alert bar; not in the nav |
| `/portal/forms` | Built [2026-11]. The portal patient's submitted forms (`listForms`), each with its questions and answers. Nav "Forms" |
| `/clinic/feedback` | Built [2026-11]. Admin, PA or doctor, not suspended (others to `/clinic`). Counts for the last 90 days; "Waiting for a follow-up" (amber and red with no follow-up, by due date, overdue marked); "Latest answers" (latest 100 of up to 500; headings as in the working tree, the commit had "To follow up" and "Recent feedback"); links to the two CSV downloads. [2026-11, `2171c59`] The description adds how many positive comments the patient agreed may be quoted are waiting to be checked (counted over the up to 500 rows read; Finding 157). Doctors see only their care team's patients (RLS). Nav "Feedback" in the Patients group |
| `/clinic/feedback/[id]` | Built [2026-11]. Same gate; 404 for a non-UUID or unreadable feedback. The answers, rating, a link to the record's forms section, and either the recorded follow-up (with the staff member's name from `get_staff_directory`) or, for amber and red without one, the follow-up form. [2026-11, `2171c59`] For green answers the patient agreed may be quoted, with a comment, a "Use as CQC evidence" card: who checked the comments and when (name from `get_staff_directory`), and `QuoteCheckForm`. Does not call `log_record_view` (Finding 151) |
| `/clinic/feedback/export` | Route handler (GET), built [2026-11]. 404 unless signed in, not suspended, and admin, PA or doctor. Reads `listAnsweredFeedback({ limit: 5000 })` through the session; **since [2026-11, `2171c59`]** `listAllAnsweredFeedback()`, every readable answer a page at a time. `?set=evidence` (the default): green answers the patient agreed may be quoted (**and, since `2171c59`, a team member has checked**), with date, score and the two comments, no name or reference. `?set=all`: every answer with clinic reference, rating, contact and quote choices, comments, due date, follow-up date and note. CSV with a byte-order mark, CRLF lines, every cell quoted and a leading `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, tab or CR neutralised with `'`; `Cache-Control: no-store`; file `feedback-<set>-<date>.csv`. Not logged anywhere (Finding 152). **Since [2026-11, `2171c59`]** the route calls `log_feedback_export` with the set and the number of data rows before responding, and returns 500 "The download could not be logged, so it was not made." if that fails |
| `/clinic/hr` | Built [2026-11, `059f927` on `origin/dev`]. Any staff member, not suspended. A card with an "Open BrightHR" link to `https://app.brighthr.com/` in a new tab (BrightHR refuses to be framed). No data, no integration. Nav "BrightHR" |

Every other nav entry (patient: Lab orders, Upload results, Prescriptions, Appointments (built since [2026-11]), Ask the clinic, Letters and documents, Privacy and consent; clinic: Lab orders, Safeguarding alerts; the clinic Patient list is built since [2026-11]) is shown disabled with a "Coming soon" tooltip. [2026-11, `feat/finalise-points`] Still disabled: patient Lab orders, Upload results and Prescriptions, clinic Lab orders. [2026-11, `feat/repeat-prescriptions`] Patient Prescriptions now links to `/portal/prescriptions`, and the clinic nav gains Prescriptions (`/clinic/prescriptions`, doctors, PAs and admins only). The patient home's running-low card now warns 21 days ahead (was 14) and its "Request a repeat" button links to `/portal/prescriptions/new`, shown only when the portal person can act (uncommitted [2026-11]).

**Results server actions** (`lib/results/actions.ts`) [2026-11, uncommitted]: `enterReport` (PA, doctor or, since [2026-11], nurse; Zod-checked; attempt limit `results-entry` first; an optional file is checked by its first bytes, stored in the `documents` bucket at `<patient id>/<uuid>.<ext>`, inserted as a `documents` row of kind `lab_report`, source `staff_upload`, shared with the patient, registered through `register_import`; then `enter_report`; redirects to `/clinic/results/<id>`. If the entry fails after the upload, the file stays in the queue), `saveComments` (doctor; updates `results.doctor_comment` row by row, 1,000 characters each, under RLS) and `releaseReport` (doctor; calls `release_report`; revalidates the clinic and portal layouts; then `tellPatientByEmail` sends the "A new result is ready" email, `resultReadyEmail` through `lib/email/send.ts`, with a link to `${SITE_URL}/portal/results` and no value or verdict. It reads the patient's sign-in email from `profiles` with the service-role client, only after the release has passed the database checks, and skips patients with no account or a suspended one. It also reads `notification_preferences` (topic `results`, channel `email`) with the service-role client and skips the email if `enabled` is false; no saved row counts as on. A failed or unconfigured email never undoes the release: the doctor is told, and the in-portal notice is already saved) [2026-11]. **Changed [2026-11, `feat/clinic-build-2`]:** `tellPatientByEmail` now calls `notifyPatient` (topic `results`) with `resultReadyEmail` and a WhatsApp text ("a doctor … has shared a new result with you. Sign in to see it: <link>"), so the preference and address checks live there; it still skips a patient with no account. A suspended account is reported to the doctor as "switched results emails off" (Finding 95). No migration change. [2026-11, `2847f26`] Now "Their account is not active, so they have not been emailed."; the link uses `getSiteUrl()`. Role checks in the actions are a first gate; the functions and RLS decide. [2026-11, `feat/cqc-audit`] `releaseReport` now reads `outcome` and `actionNote` and checks them with a Zod discriminated union (`no_action`, or `action` with 1 to 2,000 characters after trimming); a missing or unknown outcome gives "Sign the report off: action or no action." It passes `_outcome` and, for an action, `_action_note` to `release_report`. Success says "The report has been signed off and released". [2026-11, `feat/lab-webhook`, uncommitted] `matchLabFile(_state, formData)`: PA or admin, not suspended (else "Only a PA or an admin can match a file."); Zod on `importId` and `patientId` (uuids); rate limit `results-entry` per user; reads the waiting import's document path through the user's session (`status needs_match`); moves the file in the `documents` bucket to `<patient id>/<uuid>.pdf` with the **admin client** (the only service-role step), then calls `match_import_to_patient` through the session; if the database refuses, the file is moved back. Revalidates `/clinic/results`.

**Calendar server actions** (`lib/calendar/actions.ts`) [2026-11, uncommitted]. Each starts with `requireStaff()` (no session, suspended or non-staff goes to `/clinic`) and acts only for that person; the form carries an event id at most, never a user id. Google is called with the person's own token, read with the service role. No attempt limit. A token Google no longer accepts clears the connection and returns "Your Google Calendar is no longer connected. Connect it again to carry on."; any other failure returns "Google did not accept that. Please try again." and logs the step. Each success revalidates `/clinic/calendar`.

| Action | What it does |
|---|---|
| `addEvent` | Zod: title 1 to 200 characters, a real date, start and end `HH:MM` with the end after the start on the same day, place up to 300, notes up to 4,000. Creates a timed event in the person's `primary` calendar, Europe/London |
| `changeEvent` | Same checks plus an event id of 1 to 1,024 of `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`; PATCHes title, times, place and notes, keeping fields the screen does not show |
| `removeEvent` | Event id as above; deletes the event (already gone at Google counts as done) |
| `disconnectCalendar` | Revokes the token at Google (best effort) and calls `remove_google_calendar_connection`; the person's events stay in Google |

**Care team, note, education and wearable server actions** [2026-11, `feat/clinic-decisions-nov`]. All write through the person's own session (no service role), so RLS and the triggers decide; the role checks in code are a first gate that redirects. No attempt limits. Each revalidates the page it changes.

| File | Action | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `lib/patients/actions.ts` | `addToCareTeam` | PA, admin or doctor, not suspended (else `/clinic`). Zod: patient and staff UUIDs, relationship one of `primary_doctor`, `doctor`, `nurse`, `pa`, `health_coach`. Inserts a `care_team` row with `since` = London-time today. 23505 gives "This patient already has a primary doctor. …" |
| | `removeFromCareTeam` | Same gate. Sets `until` = London-time today on a current row (`until` empty); the row is kept as history |
| | `updatePatientContact` | PA only, not suspended (else `/clinic`). Zod: patient UUID; address line 1 and 2 up to 200, town or city up to 100, postcode up to 20, country up to 100, phone up to 40 (all trimmed; an empty box clears the column). Also [2026-11, third pass]: contact email up to 254 characters (empty, or a valid address), sex at birth (exactly empty, `F` or `M`, chosen, never typed) and date of birth (required, `YYYY-MM-DD`, a real date, not in the future, 1900 or later). Updates `address_line1`, `address_line2`, `city`, `postcode`, `country`, `contact_phone`, `contact_email`, `sex` and `date_of_birth` through the PA's session; the policy "PAs change patient profiles" decides. No row back gives "You cannot change this patient's details." Revalidates the record. [2026-11, `signaturerx`] |
| `lib/notes/actions.ts` | `createNote` | Doctor, nurse or health coach with a staff profile. Title 1 to 200, body 1 to 50,000 (trimmed). Inserts a draft with `author_id` = own staff id; with `supersedesId` it is a correction (23505 gives "Someone is already correcting this note."). Database messages with codes 42501, 22023 and 23505 are shown; others are not |
| | `updateNote` | Same gate and checks; updates title and body; no row back gives "Only the author can change a draft, and a signed note cannot be changed." |
| | `signNote` | Calls `sign_clinical_note` |
| | `archiveDraft` | Sets `archived_at` on a draft ("Draft discarded.") |
| `lib/education/actions.ts` | `createCourse`, `updateCourse`, `addSection`, `archivePart` | Admin (`isAdmin`) with a staff profile. Title 1 to 200, description up to 4,000, position 0 to 1,000. `createCourse` redirects to the new course; `updateCourse` sets the published switch; `archivePart` archives a section or a module. [2026-11, `fc7b5e8`] `addModule` is **removed** (lessons are added with `addLesson`) |
| | `uploadCover` [2026-11, `fc7b5e8`] | Admin. A JPEG or PNG (checked by its first bytes with `fileKind`), up to 5 MB, attempt-limited under the `document-upload` action. Uploads to `courses/<course id>/cover-<uuid>.<ext>` through the person's session, then sets `cover_path`; the old image stays |
| | `renameSection`, `addLesson` [2026-11, `fc7b5e8`] | Admin. `addLesson` inserts a **draft** lesson with an empty document at the end of the section and redirects to its editor |
| | `saveLesson` [2026-11, `fc7b5e8`] | Admin. Title 1 to 200, video link `https://` up to 2,000 or none, published switch, and the editor's JSON (up to 400,000 characters), parsed and passed through `cleanLessonDoc` (null gives "The lesson text is too long."). Updates title, `video_url`, `content` and `published`; no row back is a failure. `body` is left as it was |
| | `moveItem` [2026-11, `fc7b5e8`] | Admin. A plain form action (no state). Reads the current outline, swaps a section or lesson one place up or down (a lesson at the edge of its section moves into the neighbouring one) and calls `reorder_course`. Errors are ignored (Finding 159) |
| | `uploadLessonFile`, `archiveLessonFile` [2026-11, `fc7b5e8`] | Admin. A PDF, JPEG or PNG by its first bytes, up to 10 MB (`MAX_FILE_BYTES`), attempt-limited under `document-upload`; the name is stripped of control characters and cut to 200. Uploads to `files/<lesson id>/<uuid>.<ext>` and inserts the `education_files` row with `created_by` = own staff id. Archive sets `archived_at` |
| | `completeLesson` [2026-11, `fc7b5e8`] | Any signed-in person (else `/login`), not suspended, who can act for the portal patient (`getPortalPatientId`). Inserts `education_progress` with `completed_by` = the user; 23505 (already done) is treated as success. Redirects to the next lesson, or back with `saved=0` on another error (Finding 158) |
| | `assignCourse`, `unassignCourse` | Any staff member (`isStaff`) with a staff profile; RLS limits it to patients in scope. Assign inserts with `assigned_by` = own staff id (23505: "This course is already on the patient's portal."); unassign sets `archived_at` |
| `lib/wearables/actions.ts` | `addReading` | A signed-in person with a patient view, not suspended (else `/portal`). Zod: patient UUID, a metric from `WEARABLE_METRICS`, a number of up to 7 digits and 3 decimals (a comma is read as a point), a date from 2000-01-01 to London-time today, device up to 80. Then the metric's range. Inserts with `entered_by` = the user; RLS requires `can_act_for` |
| | `archiveReading` | Same gate; archives a reading the person entered; no row back gives "Only the person who entered a reading can remove it." |

[2026-11, `feat/clinic-build-2`] `createNote` no longer takes `supersedesId` (no corrections); its success message is "Draft saved. Sign it when it is final." and `signNote` says "Signed. The note is now final."

[2026-11, `feat/cqc-audit`] `createNote`, `updateNote` and `signNote` also read the four checkboxes (`allOptionsDiscussed`, `customisedOptionsDiscussed`, `niceOptionsDiscussed`, `sideEffectsDiscussed`; ticked when the value is `on`) and `optionsOffered` (trimmed; empty becomes null; over 5,000 characters gives "Use 5,000 characters or fewer for the options offered.") and write them with the title and body. `signNote` saves them before calling `sign_clinical_note`, so what is signed is what was on the form.

**Booking, guardian and recording server actions** [2026-11, `feat/clinic-build-2`]. No attempt limits on any of them. Database messages with codes 42501, 22023 and 23505 are shown; others give a generic message.

| File | Action | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `lib/booking/actions.ts` | `bookAppointment` | Any signed-in, not-suspended person. Zod: patient, staff and type UUIDs, `startsAt` an ISO date-time with offset, optional location UUID, `back` portal or clinic. Calls `book_appointment` through the person's session, so the database decides. Then `afterChange` (below), then redirects to `/portal/appointments?booked=1` or `/clinic/patients/<id>?booked=1` |
| | `cancelAppointment` | Staff, not suspended (else `/clinic`). Calls `cancel_appointment` through the session; then `afterChange` with `cancelled`; "Appointment cancelled. The patient has been told." (said whatever the delivery outcome) |
| | `afterChange` (internal) | Service role. For a `clinic_hub` appointment whose clinician has connected Google: on booking, creates an event in the clinician's own calendar titled "<type> · <clinic reference>" (never the patient's name) and stores its id through `set_appointment_calendar_event`; on cancelling, deletes that event. Then `notifyPatient` (topic `appointments`) with `appointmentEmail` and a WhatsApp text. Needs `getEmailEnv()` for `SITE_URL` (Finding 94). Every failure is logged and never undoes the booking |
| `lib/booking/settings-actions.ts` | `addAppointmentType`, `setTypeActive` | Any staff member passes the code gate; RLS lets only admins write (42501 gives "Only an admin can add appointment types."). Name 1 to 120, 5 to 480 minutes, clinic or video, bookable by patients (checkbox). No description field on the form |
| | `addHours`, `removeHours` | Any staff member passes the code gate; RLS allows admins, PAs and the clinician for their own hours. Weekday 1 to 7, `HH:MM` start and end with the end after the start, optional location. Remove sets `active` false |
| `lib/patients/guardian-actions.ts` | `removeGuardian` | PA or admin, not suspended (else `/clinic`). Sets `can_view` and `can_act` false on the link through the session; no row back gives an error. "Guardian removed. They no longer see this patient's account." No email to the guardian or patient |
| `lib/recordings/actions.ts` | `startRecordingUpload(patientId, mimeType)` | Called directly from the Client recorder (not a form). Doctor, nurse or health coach with a staff profile, not suspended; transcription configured; type webm, ogg or mp4. Inserts the `call_recordings` row through the session (RLS: patient in scope, `recorded_by` own), then `createSignedUploadUrl` for `<patient>/<uuid>.<ext>` through the session; returns the recording id and the URL |
| | `sendForTranscription(recordingId)` | Same gate; reads the row through the session and requires `recorded_by` = own staff id. With the service role, a one-hour signed read URL, `submitTranscription`, then `recording_sent`; on any error `recording_failed` and "The recording is saved, but could not be sent…" |
| | `checkRecording` | Form action. Same gate; reads the row through the session (any writer in scope, not only the recorder, though the screen shows the button to the recorder only). A failed recording is sent again (which then needs the recorder); otherwise `completeRecording`: "ready as a draft note", "Still being transcribed" or "failed" |

**Changes to booking [2026-11].** `17b2f43`: `bookAppointment` re-checks the clinician's Google Calendar (`busyInGoogle`) just before calling `book_appointment` ("That time is no longer free in the clinician's calendar…"). `29f602f`: the `booking` attempt limit, keyed by the person's email, after validation ("Too many bookings at once…"). `2847f26`: `afterChange` returns the `Delivery` (or null), uses `getSiteUrl()` instead of `getEmailEnv()`, passes a portal notice ("Appointment booked" or "An appointment was cancelled", link `/portal/appointments`), and the email and WhatsApp no longer name the type. Staff booking redirects to `?booked=told` when email or WhatsApp was sent, else `?booked=not-told`; `cancelAppointment` reports what happened: told by email and/or WhatsApp, "account is not active", "message … failed", "messages switched off", or "could not be messaged". Confirmations (book a slot, remove hours, discard a draft, sign a note, remove a guardian) use `ConfirmButton` instead of the browser's `confirm()` since `4fcb94d`.

**Diary and online booking server actions** [2026-11, `feat/clinic-diary`, `4f3c29c`].

| File | Action | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `lib/diary/actions.ts` | `moveAppointment` | Staff, not suspended (else `/clinic`). Zod: appointment and staff UUIDs, a London day and `HH:MM` time. `booking` attempt limit, keyed by the staff member's email ("Too many changes at once…"). Reads the appointment through the session (its `external_id`, `location_id` and old clinician's user id; no row gives "You cannot move this appointment."), then calls `move_appointment` through the session with the **old location kept**. Messages with codes 42501, 22023 and 23505 are shown. Then `afterChange(id, "moved", previous)`; revalidates the `/clinic/diary` layout; "Appointment moved. <delivery line>". **Changed by `49cb8b8` [2026-11]:** no longer passes a location (the database picks it from the new clinician's hours), and before the move checks the new clinician's Google Calendar with `stillFreeInGoogle` ("That time is taken in the clinician's calendar…"; an unreadable calendar does not block) |
| | `searchPatients` [2026-11, `49cb8b8`] | Staff, not suspended (else `/clinic`). Takes `q` from the posted form (not the address, so names stay out of logs and history), at most 80 characters reduced to letters, digits, spaces, apostrophes and hyphens; fewer than two letters or digits gives "Type at least two letters or numbers." Searches unarchived patients by first name, last name or clinic reference through the session (RLS: only patients the person can open), up to 25, by last name. No attempt limit |
| `lib/booking/online-actions.ts` | `bookOnline` | **No session.** A filled hidden `website` field redirects to `/book/done` without booking. [2026-11, `49cb8b8`] When `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY` is set, the `cf-turnstile-response` token (1 to 2,048 characters) is verified with Cloudflare's `siteverify` (5-second timeout; any failure refuses, "Please complete the check that you are not a robot…"), after the attempt limit; without the secret the check is skipped. The privacy checkbox message now names the terms too. Zod: type, clinician and optional location UUIDs, an ISO start with offset, names 1 to 80, an ISO date of birth, an email up to 254, the phone pattern, and the privacy checkbox ("Please confirm you have read the privacy notice."). `online-booking` attempt limit on the typed email. Re-reads the online types and clinicians (service role), checks the clinician's Google Calendar (`stillFreeInGoogle`), then calls `book_online` with **`createAdminClient()`**. Only the twelve messages `book_online` raises are shown word for word; anything else is "We could not book that…". Then `afterChange(appointmentId, "booked")` and a redirect to `/book/done`. **[2026-11, uncommitted, `fix/online-booking-confirmation`]** The `Delivery` from `afterChange` is now read: if `patientWasTold` is false (neither email nor WhatsApp `sent`, including a null delivery or a suspended account), `tellTeamPatientNotTold` gives admins and PAs a second notice before the redirect. The booker still sees `/book/done` either way |
| `lib/booking/settings-actions.ts` | `setTypeOnline` | Any staff member passes the code gate; the session's update of `bookable_online` must return a row (RLS: admins only), else the generic failure. Revalidates `/clinic/booking` and `/book` |

**Question, document, consent and record server actions** [2026-11, `feat/finalise-points`]. All write through the person's own session, so RLS and the functions decide; the role checks in code are a first gate that redirects. Each revalidates the pages it changes.

| File | Action | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `lib/questions/actions.ts` | `askQuestion` | Signed in, not suspended, with a patient view (else `/portal`). Zod: patient UUID, subject 1 to 120, body 1 to 4,000, urgency routine or important. `question` attempt limit. Inserts the question (`asked_by` = the user; RLS needs `can_act_for`). Then emails `questionAlertEmail` through Resend to `QUESTIONS_ALERT_TO`, else `info@theschoemanclinic.com`; a failure is logged and ignored. Redirects to `/portal/questions/<id>?sent=1` |
| | `replyToQuestion` | Signed in, not suspended (else `/login`). Zod: question UUID, body 1 to 4,000. `question` attempt limit. Calls `reply_to_question`; "closed" and "4,000" messages are shown, others generic. [2026-11, `09969cc`] The `from` field is gone: the function's boolean result decides. If the reply is from the clinic: reads the question through the session and calls `notifyPatient` (topic `messages`, notice "The clinic has replied" linking to the thread, `questionReplyEmail`, a WhatsApp text with no content); the reply says whether the patient was told. A portal reply alerts nobody (Finding 106) |
| | `closeQuestion` | Staff, not suspended (else `/login`). Calls `close_question`; any error gives "You cannot close this question." |
